import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, TrendingUp, Dumbbell, Calendar, Award, BarChart2 } from 'lucide-react';
import athleteService from '../services/athleteService';
import exerciseService from '../services/exerciseService';
import './AthleteProgressPage.css';

interface Exercise {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  muscle_group: string;
}

interface ProgressPoint {
  reps_done: number;
  weight_done: string;
  created_at: string;
  workout_date: string;
  plan_name: string;
  day_number: number;
}

interface PersonalBest {
  max_weight: number;
  max_reps: number;
  weight_done: number;
  reps_done: number;
  workout_date: string;
}

interface AthleteSummary {
  summary: {
    total_workouts: string;
    total_days: string;
    good_workouts: string;
    hard_workouts: string;
    last_workout: string | null;
  };
  recent: Array<{
    id: number;
    workout_date: string;
    feedback_emoji: string;
    plan_name: string;
    day_number: number;
  }>;
}

export const AthleteProgressPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [exercises, setExercises] = useState<Exercise[]>([]);
  const [selectedExerciseId, setSelectedExerciseId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState<ProgressPoint[]>([]);
  const [personalBest, setPersonalBest] = useState<PersonalBest | null>(null);
  const [summary, setSummary] = useState<AthleteSummary | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [progressLoading, setProgressLoading] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedExerciseId) {
      loadProgress();
    }
  }, [selectedExerciseId]);

  const getAthleteId = (): number => {
    const athleteId = localStorage.getItem('selectedAthleteId');
    if (!athleteId) {
      console.error('РќРµ РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅ');
      navigate('/select-user');
      return 0;
    }
    return parseInt(athleteId);
  };

  const loadData = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const athleteId = getAthleteId();
      if (!athleteId) return;
      
      // Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР¶Р°РµРј СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ
      const exercisesRes = await exerciseService.getAll();
      setExercises(exercisesRes.data.exercises || []);
      
      // Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР¶Р°РµРј СЃРІРѕРґРєСѓ
      const summaryRes = await athleteService.getAthleteSummary(athleteId);
      setSummary(summaryRes.data);
      
      // Р’С‹Р±РёСЂР°РµРј РїРµСЂРІРѕРµ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёРµ РїРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ
      if (exercisesRes.data.exercises?.length > 0) {
        setSelectedExerciseId(exercisesRes.data.exercises[0].id);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё РґР°РЅРЅС‹С…:', error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const loadProgress = async () => {
    if (!selectedExerciseId) return;
    
    setProgressLoading(true);
    try {
      const athleteId = getAthleteId();
      if (!athleteId) return;
      
      const response = await athleteService.getExerciseProgress(athleteId, selectedExerciseId, 20);
      setProgress(response.data.progress || []);
      setPersonalBest(response.data.personalBest || null);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµСЃСЃР°:', error);
    } finally {
      setProgressLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const formatDate = (dateStr: string) => {
    const date = new Date(dateStr);
    return date.toLocaleDateString('ru-RU');
  };

  // РЎРѕР·РґР°РµРј РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РґР»СЏ РіСЂР°С„РёРєР°
  const chartData = [...progress].reverse().map(point => ({
    date: formatDate(point.workout_date),
    weight: parseFloat(point.weight_done),
    reps: point.reps_done
  }));

  const maxWeight = Math.max(...chartData.map(d => d.weight), 0);

  return (
    <div className="athlete-progress-page">
      <div className="progress-header">
        <button className="back-btn" onClick={() => navigate('/athlete/dashboard')}>
          <ArrowLeft size={20} />
        </button>
        <h1>РњРѕР№ РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµСЃСЃ</h1>
      </div>

      {/* РЎРІРѕРґРєР° */}
      <div className="summary-grid">
        <div className="summary-card">
          <Calendar size={20} />
          <div className="summary-info">
            <span className="summary-value">{summary?.summary.total_workouts || 0}</span>
            <span className="summary-label">РўСЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРѕРє</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="summary-card">
          <TrendingUp size={20} />
          <div className="summary-info">
            <span className="summary-value">{summary?.summary.good_workouts || 0}</span>
            <span className="summary-label">Р›РµРіРєРёС…</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="summary-card">
          <Award size={20} />
          <div className="summary-info">
            <span className="summary-value">{summary?.summary.hard_workouts || 0}</span>
            <span className="summary-label">РўСЏР¶РµР»С‹С…</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Р’С‹Р±РѕСЂ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ */}
      <div className="exercise-selector-section">
        <label>Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёРµ:</label>
        <select
          value={selectedExerciseId || ''}
          onChange={(e) => setSelectedExerciseId(Number(e.target.value))}
          className="exercise-select"
          disabled={loading}
        >
          {exercises.map(ex => (
            <option key={ex.id} value={ex.id}>
              {ex.name} ({ex.muscle_group})
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>

      {/* Р›РёС‡РЅС‹Р№ СЂРµРєРѕСЂРґ */}
      {personalBest && (
        <div className="personal-best">
          <h3>рџЏ† Р›РёС‡РЅС‹Р№ СЂРµРєРѕСЂРґ</h3>
          <div className="record-details">
            <div className="record-item">
              <span className="record-value">{personalBest.weight_done} РєРі</span>
              <span className="record-label">РњР°РєСЃРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РІРµСЃ</span>
            </div>
            <div className="record-item">
              <span className="record-value">{personalBest.reps_done} СЂР°Р·</span>
              <span className="record-label">РњР°РєСЃРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂС‹</span>
            </div>
            <div className="record-date">
              {formatDate(personalBest.workout_date)}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Р“СЂР°С„РёРє РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµСЃСЃР° */}
{selectedExerciseId && (
  <div className="progress-chart">
    <h3>РџСЂРѕРіСЂРµСЃСЃ РІРµСЃР°</h3>
    {progressLoading ? (
      <div className="loading">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°...</div>
    ) : chartData.length > 0 ? (
      <div className="chart-container">
        <div className="chart-bars">
          {chartData.map((point, idx) => (
            <div key={idx} className="chart-bar-wrapper">
              <div
                className="chart-bar"
                style={{ height: `${(point.weight / maxWeight) * 150}px` }}
                title={`${point.date}: ${point.weight} РєРі`}
              />
              <span className="chart-label">{point.weight} РєРі</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="chart-footer">
          <span className="chart-unit">РњР°РєСЃРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РІРµСЃ (РєРі)</span>
          <span className="chart-max">{maxWeight} РєРі</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    ) : (
      <p className="empty-state">РќРµС‚ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… РїРѕ СЌС‚РѕРјСѓ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЋ</p>
    )}
  </div>
)}

      {/* РСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ РїРѕРґС…РѕРґРѕРІ */}
      {selectedExerciseId && progress.length > 0 && (
        <div className="history-table">
          <h3>РСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ РїРѕРґС…РѕРґРѕРІ</h3>
          <div className="table-header">
            <span>Р”Р°С‚Р°</span>
            <span>Р’РµСЃ (РєРі)</span>
            <span>РџРѕРІС‚РѕСЂС‹</span>
            <span>РџР»Р°РЅ</span>
          </div>
          <div className="table-body">
            {progress.slice(0, 10).map((point, idx) => (
              <div key={idx} className="table-row">
                <span>{formatDate(point.workout_date)}</span>
                <span className="weight-value">{point.weight_done}</span>
                <span>{point.reps_done}</span>
                <span className="plan-name">{point.plan_name}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

interface Exercise {
  id: number;
  exercise_id: number;
  exercise_name: string;
  muscle_group: string;
  sets_count: number;
  default_reps: number;
  default_weight: number;
  order_index: number;
  image_url?: string;
  video_url?: string;
}

interface Set {
  set_number: number;
  reps: number;
  weight: number | null;
  completed: boolean;
}

// РќРѕРІС‹Р№ С‚РёРї РґР»СЏ РїСЂРµРґС‹РґСѓС‰РµР№ С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєРё
interface PreviousWorkoutSet {
  set_number: number;
  reps_done: number;
  weight_done: number;
  is_completed: boolean;
}

interface PreviousWorkout {
  id: number;
  workout_date: string;
  plan_name: string;
  day_number: number;
  exercises: {
    exercise_id: number;
    exercise_name: string;
    sets: PreviousWorkoutSet[];
  }[];
}

/* в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ

const ProgressLineChart: React.FC<{
  data: number[];
  color?: string;
  format?: (v: number) => string;
}> = ({ data, color = '#a3e635', format }) => {
  if (!data || data.length === 0) {
    return <div className="progress-chart-empty">РќРµРґРѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С…</div>;
  }

  const fmt = format || ((v: number) => (Number.isInteger(v) ? `${v}` : v.toFixed(1)));
  const W = 320;
  const H = 150;
  const padL = 18;
  const padR = 18;
  const padTop = 30;
  const padBottom = 16;

  const max = Math.max(...data);
  const min = Math.min(...data);
  const range = max - min || 1;
  const n = data.length;
  const innerW = W - padL - padR;
  const innerH = H - padTop - padBottom;

  const xFor = (i: number) => (n === 1 ? W / 2 : padL + (innerW * i) / (n - 1));
  const yFor = (v: number) => padTop + innerH * (1 - (v - min) / range);

  const pts = data.map((v, i) => ({ x: xFor(i), y: yFor(v), v }));
  const line = pts.map((p, i) => `${i === 0 ? 'M' : 'L'}${p.x.toFixed(1)},${p.y.toFixed(1)}`).join(' ');
  const area = `${line} L${pts[pts.length - 1].x.toFixed(1)},${(H - padBottom).toFixed(1)} L${pts[0].x.toFixed(
    1
  )},${(H - padBottom).toFixed(1)} Z`;
  const gradId = `fitGrad-${color.replace('#', '')}`;

  return (
    <svg
      className="progress-line-chart"
      viewBox={`0 0 ${W} ${H}`}
      preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"
      role="img"
    >
      <defs>
        <linearGradient id={gradId} x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
          <stop offset="0%" stopColor={color} stopOpacity="0.22" />
          <stop offset="100%" stopColor={color} stopOpacity="0" />
        </linearGradient>
      </defs>
      <path d={area} fill={`url(#${gradId})`} />
      <path
        d={line}
        fill="none"
        stroke={color}
        strokeWidth="2.5"
        strokeLinejoin="round"
        strokeLinecap="round"
      />
      {pts.map((p, i) => (
        <g key={i}>
          <circle cx={p.x} cy={p.y} r="3.5" fill={color} stroke="#0a0a0c" strokeWidth="1.5" />
          <text
            x={Math.max(14, Math.min(W - 14, p.x))}
            y={p.y - 10}
            textAnchor="middle"
            fontSize="11"
            fontWeight="700"
            fill={color}
          >
            {fmt(p.v)}
          </text>
        </g>
      ))}
    </svg>
  );
};

export const AthleteWorkoutPage: React.FC = () => {

  const [timerValue, setTimerValue] = useState(180);
  const [timerActive, setTimerActive] = useState(false);
  const [timerSound, setTimerSound] = useState(true);

  const audioRef = useRef<HTMLAudioElement | null>(null);
  const [previousWorkout, setPreviousWorkout] = useState<PreviousWorkout | null>(null);

  const [weightHistory, setWeightHistory] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [repsHistory, setRepsHistory] = useState<number[]>([]);
  // РњРѕРґР°Р»РєР° РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅРёСЏ РІС‹С…РѕРґР° РёР· РЅРµР·Р°РІРµСЂС€С‘РЅРЅРѕР№ С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєРё
  const [showExitModal, setShowExitModal] = useState(false);
  // РђРєС‚СѓР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ С„Р»Р°Рі "С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєР° РЅР°С‡Р°С‚Р° Рё РЅРµ Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€РµРЅР°" вЂ” РґР»СЏ РїРµСЂРµС…РІР°С‚С‡РёРєРѕРІ РІС‹С…РѕРґР°
  const isDirtyRef = useRef(false);
  // Р—Р°РјРµРЅР° СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РЅР° С‚РµРєСѓС‰СѓСЋ С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєСѓ)
  const [showReplaceModal, setShowReplaceModal] = useState(false);
  const [replaceOptions, setReplaceOptions] = useState<LibraryExercise[]>([]);
  const [replaceLoading, setReplaceLoading] = useState(false);

  const currentExercise = exercises[currentExerciseIndex];
  const currentSets = sets[currentExercise?.id] || [];

  useEffect(() => {
    loadWorkoutData();
    initAudio();
    return () => {
      if (timerRef.current) clearInterval(timerRef.current);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (currentExercise) {
      loadPreviousWorkout(currentExercise.exercise_id);
    }
  }, [currentExercise]);

  // Р”РµСЂР¶РёРј ref РІ Р°РєС‚СѓР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРј СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРё: С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєР° РЅР°С‡Р°С‚Р° Рё РµС‰С‘ РЅРµ Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€РµРЅР°
  useEffect(() => {
    isDirtyRef.current = !loading && !!sessionId && !allExercisesCompleted();
  });

  // РџРµСЂРµС…РІР°С‚ СЃРёСЃС‚РµРјРЅРѕР№/Р¶РµСЃС‚РѕРІРѕР№ РєРЅРѕРїРєРё "РЅР°Р·Р°Рґ" Рё Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёСЏ РІРєР»Р°РґРєРё
  useEffect(() => {
    window.history.pushState(null, '', window.location.href);

    const handlePopState = () => {
      if (isDirtyRef.current) {
        // РћСЃС‚Р°С‘РјСЃСЏ РЅР° СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Рµ Рё РїРѕРєР°Р·С‹РІР°РµРј РјРѕРґР°Р»РєСѓ
        window.history.pushState(null, '', window.location.href);
        setShowExitModal(true);
      } else {
        // РўСЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєР° Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€РµРЅР°/РЅРµ РЅР°С‡Р°С‚Р° вЂ” РІС‹С…РѕРґРёРј РєР°Рє РѕР±С‹С‡РЅРѕ
        window.history.back();
      }
    };

    const handleBeforeUnload = (e: BeforeUnloadEvent) => {
      if (isDirtyRef.current) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.returnValue = '';
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('popstate', handlePopState);
    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', handleBeforeUnload);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('popstate', handlePopState);
      window.removeEventListener('beforeunload', handleBeforeUnload);
    };
  }, []);

  const initAudio = () => {
    audioRef.current = new Audio('/sounds/beep.mp3');
  };

  // Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° РїСЂРµРґС‹РґСѓС‰РµР№ С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєРё + СЃР±РѕСЂ РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёРё РїРѕРєР°Р·Р°С‚РµР»РµР№ РїРѕ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЋ
  const loadPreviousWorkout = async (exerciseId: number) => {
    const athleteId = localStorage.getItem('selectedAthleteId');
    if (!athleteId) return;

    setProgressLoading(true);
    setWeightHistory([]);
    setRepsHistory([]);
    try {
      // РћРґРёРЅ Р»С‘РіРєРёР№ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ: РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ РІС‹РїРѕР»РЅРµРЅРЅС‹С… СЃРµС‚РѕРІ РїРѕ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЋ
      // (Р±СЌРєРµРЅРґ РѕС‚РґР°С‘С‚ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё СЃРµС‚РѕРІ, РѕС‚СЃРѕСЂС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕ РґР°С‚Рµ С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєРё DESC).
      const response = await athleteService.getExerciseProgress(parseInt(athleteId), exerciseId, 100);
      const rows: any[] = response?.data?.progress || [];

      const now = new Date();
      const todayKey = `${now.getFullYear()}-${(now.getMonth() + 1)
        .toString()
        .padStart(2, '0')}-${now.getDate().toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;

      // Р“СЂСѓРїРїРёСЂСѓРµРј СЃРµС‚С‹ РїРѕ РґР°С‚Рµ С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєРё, СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏСЏ РїРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє (РґР°С‚С‹ РёРґСѓС‚ DESC).
      const byDate = new Map<string, any[]>();
      const datesDesc: string[] = [];
      for (const row of rows) {
        const dateKey = (row.workout_date || '').split('T')[0];
        if (!dateKey || dateKey === todayKey) continue; // РёСЃРєР»СЋС‡Р°РµРј СЃРµРіРѕРґРЅСЏС€РЅРёР№ (Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅС‹Р№) РґРµРЅСЊ
        if (!byDate.has(dateKey)) {
          byDate.set(dateKey, []);
          datesDesc.push(dateKey);
        }
        byDate.get(dateKey)!.push(row);
      }

      // Р”Рѕ 10 РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРёС… С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРѕРє; Р·Р° РєР°Р¶РґС‹Р№ РґРµРЅСЊ Р±РµСЂС‘Рј РјР°РєСЃРёРјСѓРјС‹.
      const recentDatesDesc = datesDesc.slice(0, 10);
      const historyDesc = recentDatesDesc.map((dateKey) => {
        const sets = byDate.get(dateKey)!;
        const maxWeight = Math.max(...sets.map((s) => Number(s.weight_done) || 0));
        const maxReps = Math.max(...sets.map((s) => Number(s.reps_done) || 0));
        return { maxWeight, maxReps };
      });

      // РҐСЂРѕРЅРѕР»РѕРіРёС‡РµСЃРєРёР№ РїРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє (СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹Рµ -> РЅРѕРІС‹Рµ) РґР»СЏ РіСЂР°С„РёРєР°.
      const historyAsc = [...historyDesc].reverse();
      setWeightHistory(historyAsc.map((h) => h.maxWeight));
      setRepsHistory(historyAsc.map((h) => h.maxReps));

      // Р‘Р»РѕРє "РџСЂРµРґС‹РґСѓС‰Р°СЏ С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєР°" вЂ” СЃР°РјР°СЏ СЃРІРµР¶Р°СЏ РґР°С‚Р°.
      if (recentDatesDesc.length > 0) {
        const lastDate = recentDatesDesc[0];
        // РЎРµС‚С‹ РїСЂРёС…РѕРґСЏС‚ DESC РїРѕ РІСЂРµРјРµРЅРё СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅРёСЏ вЂ” СЂР°Р·РІРѕСЂР°С‡РёРІР°РµРј РґР»СЏ РµСЃС‚РµСЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРѕРіРѕ РїРѕСЂСЏРґРєР°.
        const lastSets = [...byDate.get(lastDate)!].reverse();
        setPreviousWorkout({
          id: 0,
          workout_date: lastDate,
          plan_name: lastSets[0]?.plan_name || '',
          day_number: lastSets[0]?.day_number || 0,
          exercises: [
            {
              exercise_id: exerciseId,
              exercise_name: '',
              sets: lastSets.map((s, i) => ({
                set_number: i + 1,
                reps_done: Number(s.reps_done) || 0,
                weight_done: Number(s.weight_done) || 0,
                is_completed: true,
              })),
            },
          ],
        });
      } else {
        setPreviousWorkout(null);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё РїСЂРµРґС‹РґСѓС‰РµР№ С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєРё:', error);
    } finally {
      setProgressLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const loadWorkoutData = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await athleteService.getPlanDetails(Number(planId));
      const day = response.data.days.find((d: any) => d.id === Number(dayId));

      if (day) {
        setExercises(day.exercises || []);

        const initialSets: { [key: number]: Set[] } = {};
        day.exercises.forEach((ex: Exercise) => {
          initialSets[ex.id] = Array.from({ length: ex.sets_count }, (_, i) => ({
            set_number: i + 1,
            reps: ex.default_reps,
            weight: ex.default_weight === 0 ? null : ex.default_weight,
            completed: false,
          }));
        });
        setSets(initialSets);

        const athleteId = localStorage.getItem('selectedAthleteId');
        if (!athleteId) {
          console.error('РќРµ РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅ');
          return;
        }

        const sessionResponse = await athleteService.startWorkout(
          parseInt(athleteId),
          Number(planId),
          Number(dayId)
        );
        setSessionId(sessionResponse.data.id);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєРё:', error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSetComplete = async (exerciseId: number, setNumber: number, completed: boolean) => {
    if (!sessionId) return;

    const exercise = exercises.find((e) => e.id === exerciseId);
    if (!exercise) return;

    setSets((prevSets) => {
      const updatedSets = { ...prevSets };
      const setIndex = updatedSets[exerciseId]?.findIndex((s) => s.set_number === setNumber);

      if (setIndex !== -1 && setIndex !== undefined) {
        updatedSets[exerciseId][setIndex].completed = completed;

        // РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏРµРј РЅР° СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂ
        athleteService
          .logSet(sessionId, {
            exercise_id: exercise.exercise_id,
            set_number: setNumber,
            reps_done: updatedSets[exerciseId][setIndex].reps,
            weight_done: updatedSets[exerciseId][setIndex].weight,
            is_completed: completed,
          })
          .catch((error) => {
            console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёСЏ РїРѕРґС…РѕРґР°:', error);
            // РћС‚РєР°С‚ РїСЂРё РѕС€РёР±РєРµ
            updatedSets[exerciseId][setIndex].completed = !completed;
          });
      }
      return updatedSets;
    });
  };

  const handleSetChange = (
    exerciseId: number,
    setNumber: number,
    field: 'reps' | 'weight',
    value: number | null
  ) => {
    setSets((prevSets) => {
      const updatedSets = { ...prevSets };
      const setIndex = updatedSets[exerciseId]?.findIndex((s) => s.set_number === setNumber);

      if (setIndex !== -1 && setIndex !== undefined) {
        updatedSets[exerciseId][setIndex][field] = value;

        if (field === 'weight' && setIndex < updatedSets[exerciseId].length - 1) {
          for (let i = setIndex + 1; i < updatedSets[exerciseId].length; i++) {
            updatedSets[exerciseId][i][field] = value;
          }
        }
      }
      return updatedSets;
    });
  };

  const handleAddSet = async (exerciseId: number) => {
    const exercise = exercises.find((e) => e.id === exerciseId);
    if (!exercise) return;

    setSets((prevSets) => {
      const currentExerciseSets = prevSets[exerciseId] || [];
      if (currentExerciseSets.length >= 10) {
        alert('РњР°РєСЃРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ РєРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІРѕ РїРѕРґС…РѕРґРѕРІ - 10');
        return prevSets;
      }

      const lastSet = currentExerciseSets[currentExerciseSets.length - 1];
      const newSetNumber = currentExerciseSets.length + 1;
      const newSet: Set = {
        set_number: newSetNumber,
        reps: lastSet ? lastSet.reps : exercise.default_reps,
        weight: lastSet
          ? lastSet.weight !== null
            ? lastSet.weight
            : null
          : exercise.default_weight === 0
          ? null
          : exercise.default_weight,
        completed: false,
      };

      return {
        ...prevSets,
        [exerciseId]: [...currentExerciseSets, newSet],
      };
    });
  };

  const isExerciseCompleted = (exerciseId: number) => {
    const exerciseSets = sets[exerciseId] || [];
    return exerciseSets.length > 0 && exerciseSets.every((s) => s.completed);
  };

  const allExercisesCompleted = () => {
    return exercises.length > 0 && exercises.every((ex) => isExerciseCompleted(ex.id));
  };

  const startTimer = () => {
    setTimerActive(true);

    if (timerRef.current) clearInterval(timerRef.current);

    timerRef.current = setInterval(() => {
      setTimerValue((prev) => {
        if (prev <= 1) {
          setTimerActive(false);
          if (timerRef.current) clearInterval(timerRef.current);

          if (timerSound && audioRef.current) {
            audioRef.current.play().catch((e) => console.log('РћС€РёР±РєР° РІРѕСЃРїСЂРѕРёР·РІРµРґРµРЅРёСЏ Р·РІСѓРєР°:', e));
          }

          setTimerValue(timerDefault);
          return timerDefault;
        }
        return prev - 1;
      });
    }, 1000);
  };

  const pauseTimer = () => {
    setTimerActive(false);
    if (timerRef.current) clearInterval(timerRef.current);
  };

  const resetTimer = () => {
    setTimerValue(timerDefault);
    setTimerActive(false);
    if (timerRef.current) clearInterval(timerRef.current);
  };

  const handleTimerIncrease = () => {
    const newValue = Math.min(timerValue + 60, 600);
    setTimerValue(newValue);
    setTimerDefault(newValue);
  };

  const handleTimerDecrease = () => {
    const newValue = Math.max(timerValue - 60, 60);
    setTimerValue(newValue);
    setTimerDefault(newValue);
  };

  const formatTime = (seconds: number) => {
    const mins = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
    const secs = seconds % 60;
    return `${mins.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${secs.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
  };

  const handleCompleteWorkout = async () => {
    if (!sessionId) return;

    navigate('/athlete/complete', {
      state: {
        sessionId,
        planName: exercises[0]?.exercise_name,
      },
    });
  };

  // РљР»РёРє РїРѕ РєРЅРѕРїРєРµ "РЅР°Р·Р°Рґ" РІ С€Р°РїРєРµ
  const handleBackClick = () => {
    if (isDirtyRef.current) {
      setShowExitModal(true);
    } else {
      navigate(-1);
    }
  };

  // "Р”Р°" РІ РјРѕРґР°Р»РєРµ вЂ” Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€РёС‚СЊ Рё РѕС†РµРЅРёС‚СЊ (Р·Р°РїРёСЃСЊ СЃ СЌРјРѕРґР·Рё)
  const handleConfirmExit = () => {
    setShowExitModal(false);
    handleCompleteWorkout();
  };

  // "РќРµС‚" РІ РјРѕРґР°Р»РєРµ вЂ” РѕСЃС‚Р°С‚СЊСЃСЏ РЅР° СЌРєСЂР°РЅРµ РїРѕРґС…РѕРґРѕРІ
  const handleCancelExit = () => {
    setShowExitModal(false);
  };

  // Р•СЃС‚СЊ Р»Рё СѓР¶Рµ РІС‹РїРѕР»РЅРµРЅРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕРґС…РѕРґС‹ РїРѕ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЋ (С‚РѕРіРґР° Р·Р°РјРµРЅР° Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰РµРЅР°)
  const hasCompletedSets = (exerciseId: number) => {
    const exerciseSets = sets[exerciseId] || [];
    return exerciseSets.some((s) => s.completed);
  };

  // РћС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РјРѕРґР°Р»РєСѓ Р·Р°РјРµРЅС‹: РїРѕРґРіСЂСѓР¶Р°РµРј СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ С‚РѕР№ Р¶Рµ РіСЂСѓРїРїС‹ РјС‹С€С†
  const handleReplaceClick = async () => {
    if (!currentExercise) return;
    if (hasCompletedSets(currentExercise.id)) return; // РїРѕРґСЃС‚СЂР°С…РѕРІРєР°: РєРЅРѕРїРєР° Рё С‚Р°Рє РѕС‚РєР»СЋС‡РµРЅР°

    setShowReplaceModal(true);
    setReplaceLoading(true);
    setReplaceOptions([]);
    try {
      const response = await exerciseService.getAll(currentExercise.muscle_group);
      const list: LibraryExercise[] = response?.data?.exercises || [];
      // РСЃРєР»СЋС‡Р°РµРј СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ, СѓР¶Рµ РїСЂРёСЃСѓС‚СЃС‚РІСѓСЋС‰РёРµ РІ СЃРµРіРѕРґРЅСЏС€РЅРµРј РґРЅРµ (РІРєР»СЋС‡Р°СЏ С‚РµРєСѓС‰РµРµ)
      const usedExerciseIds = new Set(exercises.map((ex) => ex.exercise_id));
      setReplaceOptions(list.filter((ex) => !usedExerciseIds.has(ex.id)));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёР№ РґР»СЏ Р·Р°РјРµРЅС‹:', error);
    } finally {
      setReplaceLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Р’С‹Р±РѕСЂ Р·Р°РјРµРЅС‹: РїРѕРґРјРµРЅСЏРµРј СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёРµ РІ С‚РµРєСѓС‰РµР№ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё (РїР»Р°РЅ С‚СЂРµРЅРµСЂР° РЅРµ РјРµРЅСЏРµС‚СЃСЏ,
  // С†РµР»СЊ РїРѕ РїРѕРґС…РѕРґР°Рј/РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂР°Рј/РІРµСЃСѓ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏРµС‚СЃСЏ, РЅРѕРІС‹Рµ РїРѕРґС…РѕРґС‹ РїРёС€СѓС‚СЃСЏ РїРѕРґ РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ exercise_id)
  const handleSelectReplacement = (newExercise: LibraryExercise) => {
    setExercises((prev) =>
      prev.map((ex, idx) =>
        idx === currentExerciseIndex
          ? {
              ...ex,
              exercise_id: newExercise.id,
              exercise_name: newExercise.name,
              muscle_group: newExercise.muscle_group,
              image_url: newExercise.image_url,
              video_url: newExercise.video_url,
            }
          : ex
      )
    );
    setShowReplaceModal(false);
    setReplaceOptions([]);
  };

  const handleCloseReplace = () => {
    setShowReplaceModal(false);
    setReplaceOptions([]);
  };

  // РџРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµРј РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РґР»СЏ РѕС‚РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёСЏ РїСЂРµРґС‹РґСѓС‰РµР№ С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєРё
  const getPreviousWorkoutData = () => {
    if (!previousWorkout || previousWorkout.exercises.length === 0) return null;
    const exerciseData = previousWorkout.exercises[0];
    if (!exerciseData || exerciseData.sets.length === 0) return null;
    return exerciseData;
  };

  const previousData = getPreviousWorkoutData();

  // Р¤СѓРЅРєС†РёСЏ РґР»СЏ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёСЏ РІРµСЃР° СЃ 2 РґРµСЃСЏС‚РёС‡РЅС‹РјРё Р·РЅР°РєР°РјРё
  const formatWeight = (weight: number): string => {
    if (weight === undefined || weight === null) return '0';
    const numWeight = typeof weight === 'string' ? parseFloat(weight) : weight;
    if (isNaN(numWeight)) return '0';
    return numWeight.toFixed(2);
  };

  // РљРѕРјРїР°РєС‚РЅС‹Р№ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚ РІРµСЃР° РґР»СЏ РїРѕРґРїРёСЃРµР№ РіСЂР°С„РёРєР° (Р±РµР· Р»РёС€РЅРёС… РЅСѓР»РµР№)
  const formatWeightShort = (weight: number): string => {
    const n = Number(weight) || 0;
    return Number.isInteger(n) ? `${n}` : n.toFixed(1);
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="loading">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєРё...</div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="athlete-workout-page">
      <div className="workout-header">
        <button className="back-btn" onClick={handleBackClick}>
          <ArrowLeft size={20} />
        </button>
        <div className="workout-progress">
          <div className="progress-text">
            РЈРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёРµ {currentExerciseIndex + 1} РёР· {exercises.length}
          </div>
          <div className="progress-bar">
            <div
              className="progress-fill"
              style={{ width: `${((currentExerciseIndex + 1) / exercises.length) * 100}%` }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {currentExercise && (
        <div className="current-exercise">
          <h2>{currentExercise.exercise_name}</h2>
          <p className="muscle-group">{currentExercise.muscle_group}</p>

          <button
            className="replace-exercise-btn"
            onClick={handleReplaceClick}
            disabled={hasCompletedSets(currentExercise.id)}
          >
            <Repeat size={16} />
            Р—Р°РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёРµ
          </button>
          {hasCompletedSets(currentExercise.id) && (
            <p className="replace-hint">Р—Р°РјРµРЅР° РЅРµРґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРЅР°: СѓР¶Рµ РµСЃС‚СЊ РІС‹РїРѕР»РЅРµРЅРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕРґС…РѕРґС‹</p>
          )}

          {/* РќРћР’Р«Р™ Р‘Р›РћРљ: РџСЂРµРґС‹РґСѓС‰Р°СЏ С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєР° */}
          <div className="progress-chart-mini">
            <div className="progress-chart-header">
              <span className="progress-chart-title">РџСЂРµРґС‹РґСѓС‰Р°СЏ С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєР°</span>
              {progressLoading && <span className="progress-chart-loading">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°...</span>}
            </div>

            {previousData ? (
              <div className="previous-sets-column">
                {previousData.sets.map((set, idx) => (
                  <div key={idx} className="previous-set-item-center">
                    {set.reps_done} Г— {formatWeight(set.weight_done)} РєРі
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="progress-chart-empty">
                {progressLoading ? 'РџРѕРёСЃРє РїСЂРµРґС‹РґСѓС‰РµР№ С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєРё...' : 'РќРµС‚ РїСЂРµРґС‹РґСѓС‰РёС… С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРѕРє'}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* РќРћР’Р«Р™ Р‘Р›РћРљ: РџСЂРѕРіСЂРµСЃСЃ РїРѕ РІРµСЃСѓ */}
          <div className="progress-stat-chart">
            <div className="progress-chart-header">
              <span className="progress-chart-title">РџСЂРѕРіСЂРµСЃСЃ РїРѕ РІРµСЃСѓ (РєРі)</span>
              {progressLoading && <span className="progress-chart-loading">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°...</span>}
            </div>
            {weightHistory.length > 0 ? (
              <ProgressLineChart data={weightHistory} color="#a3e635" format={formatWeightShort} />
            ) : (
              <div className="progress-chart-empty">
                {progressLoading ? 'Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°...' : 'РќРµРґРѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С…'}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* РќРћР’Р«Р™ Р‘Р›РћРљ: РџСЂРѕРіСЂРµСЃСЃ РїРѕ РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂРµРЅРёСЏРј */}
          <div className="progress-stat-chart">
            <div className="progress-chart-header">
              <span className="progress-chart-title">РџСЂРѕРіСЂРµСЃСЃ РїРѕ РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂРµРЅРёСЏРј</span>
              {progressLoading && <span className="progress-chart-loading">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°...</span>}
            </div>
            {repsHistory.length > 0 ? (
              <ProgressLineChart data={repsHistory} color="#a3e635" />
            ) : (
              <div className="progress-chart-empty">
                {progressLoading ? 'Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°...' : 'РќРµРґРѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С…'}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {(currentExercise.image_url || currentExercise.video_url) && (
            <div className="exercise-media">
              {currentExercise.image_url && (
                <a href={currentExercise.image_url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                  рџ“· РЎРјРѕС‚СЂРµС‚СЊ С„РѕС‚Рѕ
                </a>
              )}
              {currentExercise.video_url && (
                <a href={currentExercise.video_url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                  рџЋҐ РЎРјРѕС‚СЂРµС‚СЊ РІРёРґРµРѕ
                </a>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="workout-sets-list">
        <div className="workout-sets-header">
          <span>РџРѕРґС…РѕРґ</span>
          <span>РџРѕРІС‚РѕСЂС‹</span>
          <span>Р’РµСЃ (РєРі)</span>
          <span>Р’С‹РїРѕР»РЅРµРЅРѕ</span>
        </div>

        {currentSets.map((set) => (
          <div key={set.set_number} className="workout-set-row">
            <span className="set-number">{set.set_number}</span>

            <input
              type="number"
              min="1"
              value={set.reps}
              onChange={(e) =>
                handleSetChange(currentExercise.id, set.set_number, 'reps', parseInt(e.target.value) || 1)
              }
              disabled={set.completed}
              className="set-input"
            />

            <input
              type="number"
              min="0"
              step="2.5"
              value={set.weight === null ? '' : set.weight}
              onChange={(e) => {
                const value = e.target.value === '' ? null : parseFloat(e.target.value);
                handleSetChange(currentExercise.id, set.set_number, 'weight', value);
              }}
              disabled={set.completed}
              className="set-input"
              placeholder="РІРµСЃ"
            />

            <button
              className={`set-checkbox ${set.completed ? 'completed' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => handleSetComplete(currentExercise.id, set.set_number, !set.completed)}
            >
              {set.completed ? <CheckCircle size={24} /> : <Circle size={24} />}
            </button>
          </div>
        ))}

        {currentSets.length < 10 && (
          <button className="add-set-btn" onClick={() => handleAddSet(currentExercise.id)}>
            <Plus size={20} />
            Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ РїРѕРґС…РѕРґ
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="timer-section">
        <div className="timer-display">
          <span className="timer-value">{formatTime(timerValue)}</span>
        </div>

        <div className="timer-controls">
          <button
            className={`timer-btn ${timerSound ? 'active' : ''}`}
            onClick={() => setTimerSound(!timerSound)}
            title={timerSound ? 'Р—РІСѓРє РІРєР»' : 'Р—РІСѓРє РІС‹РєР»'}
          >
            {timerSound ? <Volume2 size={20} /> : <VolumeX size={20} />}
          </button>

          <button className="timer-btn play-pause" onClick={startTimer} disabled={timerActive}>
            <Play size={20} />
          </button>

          <button className="timer-btn play-pause" onClick={pauseTimer} disabled={!timerActive}>
            <Pause size={20} />
          </button>

          <button className="timer-btn" onClick={resetTimer}>
            <RotateCcw size={20} />
          </button>

          <button className="timer-btn" onClick={handleTimerIncrease}>
            +1
          </button>

          <button className="timer-btn" onClick={handleTimerDecrease}>
            -1
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="workout-footer">
        <div className="exercise-nav">
          <button
            className="nav-btn"
            disabled={currentExerciseIndex === 0}
            onClick={() => setCurrentExerciseIndex((prev) => prev - 1)}
          >
            РџСЂРµРґС‹РґСѓС‰РµРµ
          </button>

          <button
            className="nav-btn"
            disabled={currentExerciseIndex === exercises.length - 1}
            onClick={() => setCurrentExerciseIndex((prev) => prev + 1)}
          >
            РЎР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РµРµ
          </button>
        </div>

        {allExercisesCompleted() && (
          <button className="complete-workout-btn" onClick={handleCompleteWorkout}>
            Р—Р°РІРµСЂС€РёС‚СЊ С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєСѓ
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {showExitModal && (
        <div className="exit-modal-overlay" onClick={handleCancelExit}>
          <div className="exit-modal" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
            <h3 className="exit-modal-title">Р’С‹ РЅРµ Р·Р°РєРѕРЅС‡РёР»Рё С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєСѓ</h3>
            <p className="exit-modal-text">
              РҐРѕС‚РёС‚Рµ Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€РёС‚СЊ Рё РѕС†РµРЅРёС‚СЊ С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєСѓ РёР»Рё РїСЂРѕРґРѕР»Р¶РёС‚СЊ?
            </p>
            <div className="exit-modal-actions">
              <button className="exit-modal-confirm" onClick={handleConfirmExit}>
                Р—Р°РІРµСЂС€РёС‚СЊ Рё РѕС†РµРЅРёС‚СЊ
              </button>
              <button className="exit-modal-cancel" onClick={handleCancelExit}>
                РџСЂРѕРґРѕР»Р¶РёС‚СЊ С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєСѓ
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {showReplaceModal && (
        <div className="replace-modal-overlay" onClick={handleCloseReplace}>
          <div className="replace-modal" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
            <h3 className="replace-modal-title">Р—Р°РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёРµ</h3>
            <p className="replace-modal-subtitle">
              {currentExercise?.muscle_group} В· С‚Р° Р¶Рµ РіСЂСѓРїРїР° РјС‹С€С†
            </p>

            {replaceLoading ? (
              <div className="replace-modal-empty">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°...</div>
            ) : replaceOptions.length > 0 ? (
              <div className="replace-options-list">
                {replaceOptions.map((ex) => (
                  <button
                    key={ex.id}
                    className="replace-option"
                    onClick={() => handleSelectReplacement(ex)}
                  >
                    <span className="replace-option-name">{ex.name}</span>
                    <span className="replace-option-group">{ex.muscle_group}</span>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="replace-modal-empty">РќРµС‚ РґСЂСѓРіРёС… СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёР№ СЌС‚РѕР№ РіСЂСѓРїРїС‹ РјС‹С€С†</div>
            )}

            <button className="replace-modal-cancel" onClick={handleCloseReplace}>
              РћС‚РјРµРЅР°
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import athleteService from '../services/athleteService';
import './CoachAthleteCalendarPage.css';

interface CalendarDay {
  emoji?: 'рџ‘Ќ' | 'рџ‘Ћ';
  sessions: Array<{
    id: number;
    plan_name: string;
    day_number: number;
  }>;
}

interface WorkoutDetails {
  id: number;
  plan_name: string;
  day_number: number;
  feedback_emoji: 'рџ‘Ќ' | 'рџ‘Ћ';
  completed_at: string;
  exercises: Array<{
    exercise_id: number;
    exercise_name: string;
    muscle_group: string;
    sets: Array<{
      set_number: number;
      reps_done: number;
      weight_done: number;
      is_completed: boolean;
    }>;
  }>;
}

export const CoachAthleteCalendarPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { athleteId } = useParams<{ athleteId: string }>();
  const [currentDate, setCurrentDate] = useState(new Date());
  const [calendar, setCalendar] = useState<Record<number, CalendarDay>>({});
  const [selectedDay, setSelectedDay] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [workoutDetails, setWorkoutDetails] = useState<WorkoutDetails | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [detailsLoading, setDetailsLoading] = useState(false);

  const year = currentDate.getFullYear();
  const month = currentDate.getMonth() + 1;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (athleteId) {
      loadCalendar();
    }
  }, [athleteId, year, month]);

  const loadCalendar = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      console.log('Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° РєР°Р»РµРЅРґР°СЂСЏ РґР»СЏ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР° ID:', athleteId);
      const response = await athleteService.getWorkoutCalendar(Number(athleteId), year, month);
      console.log('РћС‚РІРµС‚ РѕС‚ СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂР°:', response.data);
      console.log('РљР°Р»РµРЅРґР°СЂРЅС‹Рµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ:', response.data.calendar);
      setCalendar(response.data.calendar || {});
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('вќЊ РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё РєР°Р»РµРЅРґР°СЂСЏ:', error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDayClick = async (day: number) => {
    if (selectedDay === day) {
      setSelectedDay(null);
      setWorkoutDetails(null);
      return;
    }

    setSelectedDay(day);
    setDetailsLoading(true);
    try {
      const dateStr = `${year}-${month.toString().padStart(2, '0')}-${day.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
      const response = await athleteService.getWorkoutByDate(Number(athleteId), dateStr);
      setWorkoutDetails(response.data);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('вќЊ РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєРё:', error);
      setWorkoutDetails(null);
    } finally {
      setDetailsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const getDaysInMonth = () => {
    return new Date(year, month, 0).getDate();
  };

  const getFirstDayOfMonth = () => {
    return new Date(year, month - 1, 1).getDay();
  };

  const handlePrevMonth = () => {
    setCurrentDate(new Date(year, month - 2, 1));
    setSelectedDay(null);
    setWorkoutDetails(null);
    setCalendar({}); // РћС‡РёС‰Р°РµРј РєР°Р»РµРЅРґР°СЂСЊ
  };

  const handleNextMonth = () => {
    setCurrentDate(new Date(year, month, 1));
    setSelectedDay(null);
    setWorkoutDetails(null);
    setCalendar({}); // РћС‡РёС‰Р°РµРј РєР°Р»РµРЅРґР°СЂСЊ
  };

  const monthNames = [
    'РЇРЅРІР°СЂСЊ', 'Р¤РµРІСЂР°Р»СЊ', 'РњР°СЂС‚', 'РђРїСЂРµР»СЊ', 'РњР°Р№', 'РСЋРЅСЊ',
    'РСЋР»СЊ', 'РђРІРіСѓСЃС‚', 'РЎРµРЅС‚СЏР±СЂСЊ', 'РћРєС‚СЏР±СЂСЊ', 'РќРѕСЏР±СЂСЊ', 'Р”РµРєР°Р±СЂСЊ'
  ];

  const daysInMonth = getDaysInMonth();
  const firstDay = getFirstDayOfMonth();
  const days = [];

  // РџСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ СЏС‡РµР№РєРё РґР»СЏ РїРµСЂРІС‹С… РґРЅРµР№ РјРµСЃСЏС†Р°
  for (let i = 0; i < firstDay; i++) {
    days.push(<div key={`empty-${i}`} className="calendar-day empty" />);
  }

  // РЇС‡РµР№РєРё РґР»СЏ РґРЅРµР№ РјРµСЃСЏС†Р°
  for (let day = 1; day <= daysInMonth; day++) {
    const dayData = calendar[day];
    days.push(
      <div
        key={day}
        className={`calendar-day ${dayData ? 'has-workout' : ''} ${selectedDay === day ? 'selected' : ''}`}
        onClick={() => dayData && handleDayClick(day)}
      >
        <span className="day-number">{day}</span>
        {dayData && (
          <div className="workout-indicator">
            {dayData.emoji === 'рџ‘Ќ' ? (
              <span className="dot good" title="Р›РµРіРєРѕ">в—Џ</span>
            ) : dayData.emoji === 'рџ‘Ћ' ? (
              <span className="dot bad" title="РўСЏР¶РµР»Рѕ">в—Џ</span>
            ) : (
              <span className="dot neutral" title="РќРµС‚ РѕС†РµРЅРєРё">в—Џ</span>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="coach-calendar-page">
      <div className="calendar-header">
        <button className="back-btn" onClick={() => navigate('/coach/dashboard')}>
          <ArrowLeft size={20} />
        </button>
        <h1>РљР°Р»РµРЅРґР°СЂСЊ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР°</h1>
      </div>

      <div className="calendar-navigation">
        <button className="nav-btn" onClick={handlePrevMonth}>
          <ChevronLeft size={20} />
        </button>
        <h2>{monthNames[month - 1]} {year}</h2>
        <button className="nav-btn" onClick={handleNextMonth}>
          <ChevronRight size={20} />
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="calendar-weekdays">
        <div>Р’СЃ</div><div>РџРЅ</div><div>Р’С‚</div><div>РЎСЂ</div><div>Р§С‚</div><div>РџС‚</div><div>РЎР±</div>
      </div>

      <div className="calendar-grid">
        {days}
      </div>

      {loading && <div className="loading">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°...</div>}

      {selectedDay && workoutDetails && (
        <div className="workout-details">
          <h3>РўСЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєР° {selectedDay} {monthNames[month - 1]}</h3>
          
          {detailsLoading ? (
            <div className="loading">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°...</div>
          ) : (
            <div className="workout-info">
              <div className="workout-meta">
                <span className="plan-name">{workoutDetails.plan_name} (Р”РµРЅСЊ {workoutDetails.day_number})</span>
                <span className={`feedback-badge ${workoutDetails.feedback_emoji === 'рџ‘Ќ' ? 'good' : 'bad'}`}>
                  {workoutDetails.feedback_emoji === 'рџ‘Ќ' ? 'Р›РµРіРєРѕ' : 'РўСЏР¶РµР»Рѕ'}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="exercises-list">
                {workoutDetails.exercises.map((exercise, idx) => (
                  <div key={idx} className="exercise-card">
                    <h4>{exercise.exercise_name}</h4>
                    <p className="muscle-group">{exercise.muscle_group}</p>
                    
                    <div className="sets-grid">
                      <div className="sets-header">
                        <span>РџРѕРґС…РѕРґ</span>
                        <span>РџРѕРІС‚РѕСЂС‹</span>
                        <span>Р’РµСЃ (РєРі)</span>
                      </div>
                      {exercise.sets.map((set, setIdx) => (
                        <div key={setIdx} className="set-row">
                          <span>{set.set_number}</span>
                          <span>{set.reps_done}</span>
                          <span>{set.weight_done}</span>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, Dumbbell, Edit, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import workoutService from '../services/workoutService';
import api from '../services/api';
import './CoachAthletePlansPage.css';

interface WorkoutPlan {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  days_count: string;
  athletes_count: string;
  created_at: string;
}

export const CoachAthletePlansPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { athleteId } = useParams<{ athleteId: string }>();
  const [plans, setPlans] = useState<WorkoutPlan[]>([]);
  const [athleteName, setAthleteName] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (athleteId) {
      loadAthleteInfo();
      loadPlans();
    }
  }, [athleteId]);

  const loadAthleteInfo = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await api.get(`/users/${athleteId}`);
      const athlete = response.data.data;
      setAthleteName(`${athlete.first_name} ${athlete.last_name}`);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё РёРЅС„РѕСЂРјР°С†РёРё Рѕ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅРµ:', error);
    }
  };

  const loadPlans = async () => {
    try {
      // РџРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµРј С‚СЂРµРЅРµСЂР° СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР°
      const athleteResponse = await api.get(`/users/${athleteId}`);
      const coachId = athleteResponse.data.data.coach_id;
      
      if (coachId) {
        const response = await workoutService.getCoachPlans(coachId);
        setPlans(response.data || []);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё РїР»Р°РЅРѕРІ:', error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleEditPlan = (planId: number) => {
  navigate(`/coach/edit-plan/${planId}`);
};

  return (
    <div className="coach-plans-page">
      <div className="plans-header">
        <button className="back-btn" onClick={() => navigate('/coach/dashboard')}>
          <ArrowLeft size={20} />
        </button>
        <h1>РџР»Р°РЅС‹ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР°</h1>
      </div>

      {athleteName && (
        <div className="athlete-info-card">
          <h2>{athleteName}</h2>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="plans-list">
        {loading ? (
          <p className="loading">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°...</p>
        ) : plans.length > 0 ? (
          plans.map((plan) => (
            <div key={plan.id} className="plan-card">
              <div className="plan-icon">
                <Dumbbell size={24} />
              </div>
              <div className="plan-info">
                <h3>{plan.name}</h3>
                <p>{plan.days_count || '0'} С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРѕРє</p>
                <p className="plan-date">
                  РЎРѕР·РґР°РЅ: {new Date(plan.created_at).toLocaleDateString('ru-RU')}
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="plan-actions">
                <button 
                  className="edit-plan-btn"
                  onClick={() => handleEditPlan(plan.id)}
                  title="Р РµРґР°РєС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ РїР»Р°РЅ"
                >
                  <Edit size={18} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))
        ) : (
          <p className="empty-state">РЈ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР° РїРѕРєР° РЅРµС‚ РїР»Р°РЅРѕРІ</p>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="create-plan-action">
        <button 
          className="create-plan-btn"
          onClick={() => navigate('/coach/create-plan')}
        >
          <Dumbbell size={20} />
          РЎРѕР·РґР°С‚СЊ РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ РїР»Р°РЅ
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, TrendingUp, Dumbbell, Calendar, Award, User } from 'lucide-react';
import athleteService from '../services/athleteService';
import exerciseService from '../services/exerciseService';
import api from '../services/api';
import './CoachAthleteProgressPage.css';

interface Exercise {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  muscle_group: string;
}

interface ProgressPoint {
  reps_done: number;
  weight_done: string;
  created_at: string;
  workout_date: string;
  plan_name: string;
  day_number: number;
}

interface PersonalBest {
  max_weight: number;
  max_reps: number;
  weight_done: number;
  reps_done: number;
  workout_date: string;
}

interface AthleteSummary {
  summary: {
    total_workouts: string;
    total_days: string;
    good_workouts: string;
    hard_workouts: string;
    last_workout: string | null;
  };
  recent: Array<{
    id: number;
    workout_date: string;
    feedback_emoji: string;
    plan_name: string;
    day_number: number;
  }>;
}

interface AthleteInfo {
  id: number;
  first_name: string;
  last_name: string;
}

export const CoachAthleteProgressPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { athleteId } = useParams<{ athleteId: string }>();
  const [athlete, setAthlete] = useState<AthleteInfo | null>(null);
  const [exercises, setExercises] = useState<Exercise[]>([]);
  const [selectedExerciseId, setSelectedExerciseId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState<ProgressPoint[]>([]);
  const [personalBest, setPersonalBest] = useState<PersonalBest | null>(null);
  const [summary, setSummary] = useState<AthleteSummary | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [progressLoading, setProgressLoading] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (athleteId) {
      loadData();
    }
  }, [athleteId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedExerciseId) {
      loadProgress();
    }
  }, [selectedExerciseId]);

  const loadData = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      // Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР¶Р°РµРј РёРЅС„РѕСЂРјР°С†РёСЋ Рѕ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅРµ
      const athleteResponse = await api.get(`/users/${athleteId}`);
      setAthlete(athleteResponse.data.data);
      
      // Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР¶Р°РµРј СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ
      const exercisesRes = await exerciseService.getAll();
      setExercises(exercisesRes.data.exercises || []);
      
      // Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР¶Р°РµРј СЃРІРѕРґРєСѓ
      const summaryRes = await athleteService.getAthleteSummary(Number(athleteId));
      setSummary(summaryRes.data);
      
      // Р’С‹Р±РёСЂР°РµРј РїРµСЂРІРѕРµ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёРµ РїРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ
      if (exercisesRes.data.exercises?.length > 0) {
        setSelectedExerciseId(exercisesRes.data.exercises[0].id);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё РґР°РЅРЅС‹С…:', error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const loadProgress = async () => {
    if (!selectedExerciseId) return;
    
    setProgressLoading(true);
    try {
      const response = await athleteService.getExerciseProgress(Number(athleteId), selectedExerciseId, 20);
      setProgress(response.data.progress || []);
      setPersonalBest(response.data.personalBest || null);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµСЃСЃР°:', error);
    } finally {
      setProgressLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const formatDate = (dateStr: string) => {
    const date = new Date(dateStr);
    return date.toLocaleDateString('ru-RU');
  };

  const getExerciseName = (id: number) => {
    const exercise = exercises.find(e => e.id === id);
    return exercise?.name || 'РЈРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёРµ РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅРѕ';
  };

  // РЎРѕР·РґР°РµРј РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РґР»СЏ РіСЂР°С„РёРєР°
  const chartData = [...progress].reverse().map(point => ({
    date: formatDate(point.workout_date),
    weight: parseFloat(point.weight_done),
    reps: point.reps_done
  }));

  const maxWeight = Math.max(...chartData.map(d => d.weight), 0);

  return (
    <div className="coach-progress-page">
      <div className="progress-header">
        <button className="back-btn" onClick={() => navigate('/coach/dashboard')}>
          <ArrowLeft size={20} />
        </button>
        <h1>РџСЂРѕРіСЂРµСЃСЃ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР°</h1>
      </div>

      {/* РРЅС„РѕСЂРјР°С†РёСЏ Рѕ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅРµ */}
      {athlete && (
        <div className="athlete-info-card">
          <User size={24} />
          <div className="athlete-info">
            <h2>{athlete.first_name} {athlete.last_name}</h2>
            <p>ID: {athlete.id}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* РЎРІРѕРґРєР° */}
      <div className="summary-grid">
        <div className="summary-card">
          <Calendar size={20} />
          <div className="summary-info">
            <span className="summary-value">{summary?.summary.total_workouts || 0}</span>
            <span className="summary-label">РўСЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРѕРє</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="summary-card">
          <TrendingUp size={20} />
          <div className="summary-info">
            <span className="summary-value">{summary?.summary.good_workouts || 0}</span>
            <span className="summary-label">Р›РµРіРєРёС…</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="summary-card">
          <Award size={20} />
          <div className="summary-info">
            <span className="summary-value">{summary?.summary.hard_workouts || 0}</span>
            <span className="summary-label">РўСЏР¶РµР»С‹С…</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Р’С‹Р±РѕСЂ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ */}
      <div className="exercise-selector-section">
        <label>Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёРµ:</label>
        <select
          value={selectedExerciseId || ''}
          onChange={(e) => setSelectedExerciseId(Number(e.target.value))}
          className="exercise-select"
          disabled={loading}
        >
          {exercises.map(ex => (
            <option key={ex.id} value={ex.id}>
              {ex.name} ({ex.muscle_group})
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>

      {/* Р›РёС‡РЅС‹Р№ СЂРµРєРѕСЂРґ */}
      {personalBest && (
        <div className="personal-best">
          <h3>рџЏ† Р›РёС‡РЅС‹Р№ СЂРµРєРѕСЂРґ</h3>
          <div className="record-details">
            <div className="record-item">
              <span className="record-value">{personalBest.weight_done} РєРі</span>
              <span className="record-label">РњР°РєСЃРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РІРµСЃ</span>
            </div>
            <div className="record-item">
              <span className="record-value">{personalBest.reps_done} СЂР°Р·</span>
              <span className="record-label">РњР°РєСЃРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂС‹</span>
            </div>
            <div className="record-date">
              {formatDate(personalBest.workout_date)}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Р“СЂР°С„РёРє РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµСЃСЃР° */}
{selectedExerciseId && (
  <div className="progress-chart">
    <h3>РџСЂРѕРіСЂРµСЃСЃ РІРµСЃР°</h3>
    {progressLoading ? (
      <div className="loading">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°...</div>
    ) : chartData.length > 0 ? (
      <div className="chart-container">
        <div className="chart-bars">
          {chartData.map((point, idx) => (
            <div key={idx} className="chart-bar-wrapper">
              <div
                className="chart-bar"
                style={{ height: `${(point.weight / maxWeight) * 150}px` }}
                title={`${point.date}: ${point.weight} РєРі`}
              />
              <span className="chart-label">{point.weight} РєРі</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="chart-footer">
          <span className="chart-unit">РњР°РєСЃРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РІРµСЃ (РєРі)</span>
          <span className="chart-max">{maxWeight} РєРі</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    ) : (
      <p className="empty-state">РќРµС‚ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… РїРѕ СЌС‚РѕРјСѓ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЋ</p>
    )}
  </div>
)}

      {/* РСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ РїРѕРґС…РѕРґРѕРІ */}
      {selectedExerciseId && progress.length > 0 && (
        <div className="history-table">
          <h3>РСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ РїРѕРґС…РѕРґРѕРІ</h3>
          <div className="table-header">
            <span>Р”Р°С‚Р°</span>
            <span>Р’РµСЃ (РєРі)</span>
            <span>РџРѕРІС‚РѕСЂС‹</span>
            <span>РџР»Р°РЅ</span>
          </div>
          <div className="table-body">
            {progress.slice(0, 10).map((point, idx) => (
              <div key={idx} className="table-row">
                <span>{formatDate(point.workout_date)}</span>
                <span className="weight-value">{point.weight_done}</span>
                <span>{point.reps_done}</span>
                <span className="plan-name">{point.plan_name}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  Users, Dumbbell, Calendar, PlusCircle, CalendarDays,
  TrendingUp, UserPlus, Copy, Check, X, Share2,
} from 'lucide-react';
import api from '../services/api';
import workoutService from '../services/workoutService';
import athleteService from '../services/athleteService';
import './CoachDashboard.css';

interface Athlete {
  id: number;
  first_name: string;
  last_name?: string;
  phone?: string;
}

type ModalStep = 'form' | 'saving' | 'done';

const BOT_LINK = 'https://t.me/kablaev_team_bot';

export const CoachDashboard: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [athletes, setAthletes] = useState<Athlete[]>([]);
  const [plansCount, setPlansCount] = useState(0);
  const [totalWorkouts, setTotalWorkouts] = useState(0);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  // РњРѕРґР°Р»РєР° СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅРёСЏ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР°
  const [showModal, setShowModal] = useState(false);
  const [modalStep, setModalStep] = useState<ModalStep>('form');
  const [firstName, setFirstName] = useState('');
  const [lastName, setLastName] = useState('');
  const [createdName, setCreatedName] = useState('');
  const [athleteBotLink, setAthleteBotLink] = useState(''); // СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ СЃСЃС‹Р»РєР° СЃ ID СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР°
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState('');
  const [createdAthleteId, setCreatedAthleteId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const coachId = localStorage.getItem('selectedCoachId');
    if (coachId) {
      loadDashboard(parseInt(coachId));
    } else {
      navigate('/select-user');
    }
  }, []);

  const loadDashboard = async (coachId: number) => {
    try {
      const [athletesRes, plansRes] = await Promise.all([
        api.get(`/users/coach/${coachId}/athletes`),
        workoutService.getCoachPlans(coachId),
      ]);
      const allAthletes: Athlete[] = athletesRes.data.data || [];
      setAthletes(allAthletes);
      setPlansCount(plansRes.data.length);

      let total = 0;
      for (const a of allAthletes) {
        try {
          const s = await athleteService.getAthleteSummary(a.id);
          total += parseInt(s.data.summary.total_workouts) || 0;
        } catch { }
      }
      setTotalWorkouts(total);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё РґР°С€Р±РѕСЂРґР°:', error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
  // РЎРѕР·РґР°РЅРёРµ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР°
  // в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
  const openModal = () => {
    setFirstName('');
    setLastName('');
    setFormError('');
    setModalStep('form');
    setCopied(false);
    setShowModal(true);
  };

  const closeModal = () => setShowModal(false);

  const handleCreate = async () => {
    const trimmed = firstName.trim();
    if (!trimmed) {
      setFormError('Р’РІРµРґРёС‚Рµ РёРјСЏ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР°');
      return;
    }
    const coachId = localStorage.getItem('selectedCoachId');
    if (!coachId) return;

    setFormError('');
    setModalStep('saving');

    try {
      const { data } = await api.post('/athletes', {
        first_name: trimmed,
        last_name: lastName.trim() || undefined,
        coach_id: parseInt(coachId),
      });

      const botLink: string = data.data.botLink || BOT_LINK;
      setAthleteBotLink(botLink);
      setCreatedAthleteId(data.data.athlete?.id ?? null);

      const fullName = [trimmed, lastName.trim()].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
      setCreatedName(fullName);
      setModalStep('done');

      // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅРѕРІ
      loadDashboard(parseInt(coachId));

      // РђРІС‚РѕРјР°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРё РѕС‚РєСЂС‹РІР°РµРј СЃРёСЃС‚РµРјРЅС‹Р№ РґРёР°Р»РѕРі В«РџРѕРґРµР»РёС‚СЊСЃСЏВ»
      const shareText = `РџСЂРёРІРµС‚! РўСЂРµРЅРµСЂ РґРѕР±Р°РІРёР» С‚РµР±СЏ РІ С„РёС‚РЅРµСЃ-РїСЂРёР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёРµ. РћС‚РєСЂРѕР№ Р±РѕС‚Р° РІ Telegram Рё РЅР°Р¶РјРё РЎС‚Р°СЂС‚:`;
      if (navigator.share) {
        try {
          await navigator.share({
            title: 'РџСЂРёРіР»Р°С€РµРЅРёРµ РІ РєРѕРјР°РЅРґСѓ',
            text: shareText,
            url: botLink,
          });
        } catch { /* РѕС‚РјРµРЅРёР»Рё вЂ” РЅРёС‡РµРіРѕ СЃС‚СЂР°С€РЅРѕРіРѕ */ }
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅРёСЏ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР°:', error);
      setModalStep('form');
      setFormError('РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ СЃРѕР·РґР°С‚СЊ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР°. РџРѕРїСЂРѕР±СѓР№С‚Рµ РµС‰С‘ СЂР°Р·.');
    }
  };

  const handleShare = async () => {
    const link = athleteBotLink || BOT_LINK;
    const text = `РџСЂРёРІРµС‚! РўСЂРµРЅРµСЂ РґРѕР±Р°РІРёР» С‚РµР±СЏ РІ С„РёС‚РЅРµСЃ-РїСЂРёР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёРµ. РћС‚РєСЂРѕР№ Р±РѕС‚Р° РІ Telegram Рё РЅР°Р¶РјРё РЎС‚Р°СЂС‚:`;

    // 1. Web Share API (iOS/Android вЂ” РѕС‚РєСЂС‹РІР°РµС‚ WhatsApp, Telegram Рё РґСЂ.)
    if (navigator.share) {
      try {
        await navigator.share({ title: 'РџСЂРёРіР»Р°С€РµРЅРёРµ РІ РєРѕРјР°РЅРґСѓ', text, url: link });
        return;
      } catch { /* РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЊ РѕС‚РјРµРЅРёР» */ }
    }

    // 2. Telegram openTelegramLink (РµСЃР»Рё Р·Р°РєСЂС‹Р»Рё РґРёР°Р»РѕРі, РЅРѕ РјС‹ РІ Mini App)
    const tg = (window as any).Telegram?.WebApp;
    if (tg?.openTelegramLink) {
      tg.openTelegramLink(
        `https://t.me/share/url?url=${encodeURIComponent(link)}&text=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`
      );
      return;
    }

    // 3. Fallback вЂ” РєРѕРїРёСЂСѓРµРј РІ Р±СѓС„РµСЂ
    handleCopy();
  };

  const handleCopy = async () => {
    const link = athleteBotLink || BOT_LINK;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(link);
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2500);
    } catch { }
  };

  return (
    <div className="coach-dashboard">
      <div className="dashboard-header">
        <h1>РџР°РЅРµР»СЊ С‚СЂРµРЅРµСЂР°</h1>
        <p>РЈРїСЂР°РІР»СЏР№С‚Рµ С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєР°РјРё Рё СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР°РјРё</p>
      </div>

      <div className="stats-grid">
        <div className="stat-card">
          <Users size={24} />
          <div className="stat-info">
            <span className="stat-value">{athletes.length}</span>
            <span className="stat-label">РЎРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅРѕРІ</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="stat-card">
          <Dumbbell size={24} />
          <div className="stat-info">
            <span className="stat-value">{plansCount}</span>
            <span className="stat-label">РџР»Р°РЅРѕРІ</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="stat-card">
          <Calendar size={24} />
          <div className="stat-info">
            <span className="stat-value">{totalWorkouts}</span>
            <span className="stat-label">РўСЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРѕРє</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="action-buttons">
        <button className="action-button primary" onClick={() => navigate('/coach/exercises')}>
          <Dumbbell size={20} />
          Р‘РёР±Р»РёРѕС‚РµРєР° СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёР№
        </button>
        <button className="action-button secondary" onClick={() => navigate('/coach/create-plan')}>
          <PlusCircle size={20} />
          РЎРѕР·РґР°С‚СЊ РїР»Р°РЅ

                  <p>{athlete.phone || 'РќРµС‚ С‚РµР»РµС„РѕРЅР°'}</p>

                  <button

                    <CalendarDays size={18} />

                  <button

                {modalStep === 'done' ? 'РЎРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅ РґРѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅ' : 'РќРѕРІС‹Р№ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅ'}
              </h3>
              <button className="modal-close-btn" onClick={closeModal}>
                <X size={20} />
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* в”Ђв”Ђ Р¤РѕСЂРјР° в”Ђв”Ђ */}
            {modalStep === 'form' && (
              <div className="modal-body">
                <p className="modal-hint">
                  Р’РІРµРґРёС‚Рµ РёРјСЏ Рё С„Р°РјРёР»РёСЋ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР°. РџРѕСЃР»Рµ СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ РІС‹ СЃРјРѕР¶РµС‚Рµ РѕС‚РїСЂР°РІРёС‚СЊ РµРјСѓ СЃСЃС‹Р»РєСѓ РЅР° Р±РѕС‚Р°.

            {/* в”Ђв”Ђ РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёРµ в”Ђв”Ђ */}

              <div className="modal-body modal-saving">
                <div className="modal-spinner" />
                <p>РЎРѕР·РґР°С‘Рј РїСЂРѕС„РёР»СЊвЂ¦</p>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* в”Ђв”Ђ РЈСЃРїРµС… + С€Р°СЂР° в”Ђв”Ђ */}
            {modalStep === 'done' && (
              <div className="modal-body">
                <div className="done-athlete-name">{createdName}</div>
                <p className="modal-hint">
                  РўРµРїРµСЂСЊ РѕС‚РїСЂР°РІСЊС‚Рµ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅСѓ СЃСЃС‹Р»РєСѓ РЅР° Р±РѕС‚Р°. РћРЅ РЅР°Р¶РјС‘С‚ <strong>РЎС‚Р°СЂС‚</strong> вЂ” Рё РїСЂРёР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёРµ РѕС‚РєСЂРѕРµС‚СЃСЏ.

                <div className="invite-code-block">
                  <p className="invite-code-label">РЎРѕРѕР±С‰Рё СЌС‚РѕС‚ РєРѕРґ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅСѓ</p>
                  <div className="invite-code-value">{createdAthleteId ?? 'вЂ”'}</div>
                </div>

                <div className="done-actions">
                  <button className="share-btn" onClick={handleShare}>
                    <Share2 size={18} />
                    РћС‚РїСЂР°РІРёС‚СЊ
                  </button>
                  <button className="copy-btn" onClick={handleCopy}>
                    {copied
                      ? <><Check size={16} /> РЎРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРѕ</>
                      : <><Copy size={16} /> РљРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ</>}
                  </button>
                </div>
                <button className="modal-add-more-btn" onClick={openModal}>
                  + Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ РµС‰С‘ РѕРґРЅРѕРіРѕ
                </button>
              </div>
            )}

          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
