import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, Plus, Save, Users } from 'lucide-react';
import type { WorkoutDay, DayExercise } from '../types/workout';
import workoutService from '../services/workoutService';
import type { Exercise } from '../services/exerciseService';
import exerciseService from '../services/exerciseService';
import api from '../services/api';
import { ExerciseSelector } from '../components/ExerciseSelector';
import { PlanExerciseItem } from '../components/PlanExerciseItem';
import './CreatePlanPage.css';

export const CoachEditPlanPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { planId } = useParams<{ planId: string }>();
  const [planName, setPlanName] = useState('');
  const [days, setDays] = useState<{ number: number; exercises: DayExercise[] }[]>([]);
  const [currentDay, setCurrentDay] = useState(1);
  const [showExerciseSelector, setShowExerciseSelector] = useState(false);
  const [athletes, setAthletes] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedAthletes, setSelectedAthletes] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [showAthleteSelector, setShowAthleteSelector] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  const getCoachId = (): number | null => {
    const coachId = localStorage.getItem('selectedCoachId');
    if (!coachId) {
      console.error('РќРµ РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅ С‚СЂРµРЅРµСЂ');
      return null;
    }
    return parseInt(coachId);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (planId) {
      loadPlanData();
      loadAthletes();
    }
  }, [planId]);

  const loadPlanData = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await workoutService.getPlanById(Number(planId));
      const plan = response.data;
      setPlanName(plan.name);
      
      const daysData = plan.days || [];
      setDays(daysData.map((day: WorkoutDay) => ({
        number: day.day_number,
        exercises: day.exercises.filter((ex: any) => ex && ex.id).map((ex: any) => ({
          ...ex,
          day_id: day.id
        }))
      })));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё РїР»Р°РЅР°:', error);
      alert('РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РёС‚СЊ РїР»Р°РЅ');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const loadAthletes = async () => {
    try {
      const coachId = getCoachId();
      if (!coachId) return;

      const response = await api.get(`/users/coach/${coachId}/athletes`);
      setAthletes(response.data.data || []);
      
      // Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР¶Р°РµРј СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅРѕРІ, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Рј РЅР°Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅ СЌС‚РѕС‚ РїР»Р°РЅ
      const athletesResponse = await workoutService.getPlanAthletes(Number(planId));
      const assignedAthletes = athletesResponse.data || [];
      setSelectedAthletes(assignedAthletes.map((a: any) => a.id));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅРѕРІ:', error);
    }
  };

  const handleUpdatePlan = async () => {
    if (!planName.trim()) {
      alert('Р’РІРµРґРёС‚Рµ РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ РїР»Р°РЅР°');
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    try {
      await workoutService.updatePlan(Number(planId), { name: planName });
      alert('РџР»Р°РЅ СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅ!');
      navigate('/coach/dashboard');
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёСЏ РїР»Р°РЅР°:', error);
      alert('РћС€РёР±РєР° РїСЂРё РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРё РїР»Р°РЅР°');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const handleAddExercise = async (exercise: Exercise) => {
    if (!planId) {
      console.error('planId РЅРµ РѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»РµРЅ');
      return;
    }

    try {
      const currentDayData = days.find(d => d.number === currentDay);
      if (!currentDayData) {
        console.error('Р”РµРЅСЊ РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ');
        return;
      }

      let dayId = currentDayData.exercises[0]?.day_id;
      
      if (!dayId) {
        const daysResponse = await workoutService.getPlanDays(Number(planId));
        const dayFromApi = daysResponse.data.find((d: any) => d.day_number === currentDay);
        if (dayFromApi) {
          dayId = dayFromApi.id;
        } else {
          console.error('РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РїРѕР»СѓС‡РёС‚СЊ ID РґРЅСЏ');
          return;
        }
      }

      await workoutService.addExerciseToDay(dayId, {
        exercise_id: exercise.id,
        sets_count: 3,
        default_reps: 10,
        default_weight: null,  // в†ђ РјРµРЅСЏРµРј 0 РЅР° null
        order_index: currentDayData.exercises.length
      });

      const response = await workoutService.getPlanById(Number(planId));
      const updatedDays = response.data.days || [];
      setDays(updatedDays.map((day: WorkoutDay) => ({
        number: day.day_number,
        exercises: day.exercises.filter((ex: any) => ex && ex.id).map((ex: any) => ({
          ...ex,
          day_id: day.id
        }))
      })));
      
      setShowExerciseSelector(false);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° РґРѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅРёСЏ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ:', error);
      alert('РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РґРѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёРµ');
    }
  };

  const handleUpdateExercise = async (exerciseId: number, data: any) => {
    try {
      await workoutService.updateDayExercise(exerciseId, data);
      
      const response = await workoutService.getPlanById(Number(planId));
      const updatedDays = response.data.days || [];
      setDays(updatedDays.map((day: WorkoutDay) => ({
        number: day.day_number,
        exercises: day.exercises.filter((ex: any) => ex && ex.id).map((ex: any) => ({
          ...ex,
          day_id: day.id
        }))
      })));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёСЏ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ:', error);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteExercise = async (exerciseId: number) => {
    if (!confirm('РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёРµ РёР· РґРЅСЏ?')) return;
    
    try {
      await workoutService.deleteDayExercise(exerciseId);
      
      const response = await workoutService.getPlanById(Number(planId));
      const updatedDays = response.data.days || [];
      setDays(updatedDays.map((day: WorkoutDay) => ({
        number: day.day_number,
        exercises: day.exercises.filter((ex: any) => ex && ex.id).map((ex: any) => ({
          ...ex,
          day_id: day.id
        }))
      })));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° СѓРґР°Р»РµРЅРёСЏ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ:', error);
    }
  };

  const handleMoveUp = async (dayNumber: number, index: number) => {
    if (index === 0) return;
    
    const day = days.find(d => d.number === dayNumber);
    if (!day) return;
    
    const exercises = [...day.exercises];
    [exercises[index-1], exercises[index]] = [exercises[index], exercises[index-1]];
    
    if (exercises[0]?.day_id) {
      try {
        const orderData = exercises.map((ex, i) => ({ id: ex.id, order_index: i }));
        await workoutService.reorderExercises(exercises[0].day_id, orderData);
        
        const updatedDays = [...days];
        const dayIndex = updatedDays.findIndex(d => d.number === dayNumber);
        if (dayIndex !== -1) {
          updatedDays[dayIndex].exercises = exercises;
          setDays(updatedDays);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° РїРµСЂРµРјРµС‰РµРЅРёСЏ:', error);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleMoveDown = async (dayNumber: number, index: number) => {
    const day = days.find(d => d.number === dayNumber);
    if (!day || index === day.exercises.length - 1) return;
    
    const exercises = [...day.exercises];
    [exercises[index], exercises[index+1]] = [exercises[index+1], exercises[index]];
    
    if (exercises[0]?.day_id) {
      try {
        const orderData = exercises.map((ex, i) => ({ id: ex.id, order_index: i }));
        await workoutService.reorderExercises(exercises[0].day_id, orderData);
        
        const updatedDays = [...days];
        const dayIndex = updatedDays.findIndex(d => d.number === dayNumber);
        if (dayIndex !== -1) {
          updatedDays[dayIndex].exercises = exercises;
          setDays(updatedDays);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° РїРµСЂРµРјРµС‰РµРЅРёСЏ:', error);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleAddDay = async () => {
    if (days.length >= 10) return;
    
    try {
      await workoutService.addDay(Number(planId), days.length + 1);
      const response = await workoutService.getPlanById(Number(planId));
      const updatedDays = response.data.days || [];
      setDays(updatedDays.map((day: WorkoutDay) => ({
        number: day.day_number,
        exercises: day.exercises.filter((ex: any) => ex && ex.id).map((ex: any) => ({
          ...ex,
          day_id: day.id
        }))
      })));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° РґРѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅРёСЏ РґРЅСЏ:', error);
    }
  };

  const handleAssignPlans = async () => {
    if (selectedAthletes.length === 0) {
      alert('Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅРѕРІ');
      return;
    }

    try {
      for (const athleteId of selectedAthletes) {
        await workoutService.assignToAthlete(Number(planId), athleteId);
      }
      alert('РџР»Р°РЅ СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ РЅР°Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР°Рј!');
      setShowAthleteSelector(false);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° РЅР°Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёСЏ РїР»Р°РЅР°:', error);
      alert('РћС€РёР±РєР° РїСЂРё РЅР°Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРё РїР»Р°РЅР°');
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="loading">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° РїР»Р°РЅР°...</div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="create-plan-page">
      <div className="page-header">
        <button className="back-btn" onClick={() => navigate('/coach/dashboard')}>
          <ArrowLeft size={20} />
        </button>
        <h1>Р РµРґР°РєС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ РїР»Р°РЅР°</h1>
      </div>

      <div className="plan-setup">
        <input
          type="text"
          placeholder="РќР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ РїР»Р°РЅР°"
          value={planName}
          onChange={(e) => setPlanName(e.target.value)}
          className="plan-name-input"
        />
        <button 
          className="create-btn"
          onClick={handleUpdatePlan}
          disabled={saving}
        >
          <Save size={20} />
          {saving ? 'РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёРµ...' : 'РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёСЏ'}
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="days-tabs">
        {days.map((day) => (
          <button
            key={day.number}
            className={`day-tab ${currentDay === day.number ? 'active' : ''}`}
            onClick={() => setCurrentDay(day.number)}
          >
            Р”РµРЅСЊ {day.number}
          </button>
        ))}
        {days.length < 10 && (
          <button className="add-day-btn" onClick={handleAddDay}>
            <Plus size={18} />
            Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ РґРµРЅСЊ
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="day-content">
        <div className="exercises-list">
          {days[currentDay-1]?.exercises.map((exercise, index) => (
            <PlanExerciseItem
              key={exercise.id}
              exercise={exercise}
              onUpdate={handleUpdateExercise}
              onDelete={handleDeleteExercise}
              onMoveUp={() => handleMoveUp(currentDay, index)}
              onMoveDown={() => handleMoveDown(currentDay, index)}
              showMoveButtons={days[currentDay-1]?.exercises.length > 1}
            />
          ))}

          <button
            className="add-exercise-btn"
            onClick={() => setShowExerciseSelector(true)}
          >
            <Plus size={20} />
            Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёРµ
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="plan-actions">
        <button
          className="assign-btn"
          onClick={() => setShowAthleteSelector(true)}
        >
          <Users size={20} />
          РќР°Р·РЅР°С‡РёС‚СЊ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР°Рј
        </button>
      </div>

      {showExerciseSelector && (
        <ExerciseSelector
          onSelect={handleAddExercise}
          onClose={() => setShowExerciseSelector(false)}
          selectedExerciseIds={days[currentDay-1]?.exercises.map(ex => ex.exercise_id) || []}
        />
      )}

      {showAthleteSelector && (
        <div className="selector-overlay" onClick={() => setShowAthleteSelector(false)}>
          <div className="selector-content" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
            <div className="selector-header">
              <h2>Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅРѕРІ</h2>
              <button className="close-btn" onClick={() => setShowAthleteSelector(false)}>
                <ArrowLeft size={20} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="athlete-list">
              {athletes.map(athlete => (
                <label key={athlete.id} className="athlete-checkbox">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={selectedAthletes.includes(athlete.id)}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      if (e.target.checked) {
                        setSelectedAthletes([...selectedAthletes, athlete.id]);
                      } else {
                        setSelectedAthletes(selectedAthletes.filter(id => id !== athlete.id));
                      }
                    }}
                  />
                  <span>{athlete.first_name} {athlete.last_name}</span>
                </label>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="selector-actions">
              <button className="cancel-btn" onClick={() => setShowAthleteSelector(false)}>
                РћС‚РјРµРЅР°
              </button>
              <button className="save-btn" onClick={handleAssignPlans}>
                РќР°Р·РЅР°С‡РёС‚СЊ
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, Plus, Save, Users } from 'lucide-react';
import type { WorkoutDay, DayExercise } from '../types/workout';
import workoutService from '../services/workoutService';
import type { Exercise } from '../services/exerciseService';
import exerciseService from '../services/exerciseService';
import api from '../services/api';
import { ExerciseSelector } from '../components/ExerciseSelector';
import { PlanExerciseItem } from '../components/PlanExerciseItem';
import './CreatePlanPage.css';

export const CreatePlanPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [planName, setPlanName] = useState('');
  const [days, setDays] = useState<{ number: number; exercises: DayExercise[] }[]>([]);
  const [currentDay, setCurrentDay] = useState(1);
  const [showExerciseSelector, setShowExerciseSelector] = useState(false);
  const [planId, setPlanId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [athletes, setAthletes] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedAthletes, setSelectedAthletes] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [showAthleteSelector, setShowAthleteSelector] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  // РџРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµРј ID С‚СЂРµРЅРµСЂР° РёР· localStorage
  const getCoachId = (): number | null => {
    const coachId = localStorage.getItem('selectedCoachId');
    if (!coachId) {
      console.error('РќРµ РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅ С‚СЂРµРЅРµСЂ');
      return null;
    }
    return parseInt(coachId);
  };

  // Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР¶Р°РµРј СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅРѕРІ С‚СЂРµРЅРµСЂР°
  useEffect(() => {
    loadAthletes();
  }, []);

  const loadAthletes = async () => {
    try {
      const coachId = getCoachId();
      if (!coachId) return;

      const response = await api.get(`/users/coach/${coachId}/athletes`);
      const data = response.data;
      
      console.log('Р”Р°РЅРЅС‹Рµ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅРѕРІ:', data.data);
      setAthletes(data.data || []);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅРѕРІ:', error);
    }
  };

  // РЎРѕР·РґР°РЅРёРµ РЅРѕРІРѕРіРѕ РїР»Р°РЅР°
  const handleCreatePlan = async () => {
    if (!planName.trim()) {
      alert('Р’РІРµРґРёС‚Рµ РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ РїР»Р°РЅР°');
      return;
    }

    const coachId = getCoachId();
    if (!coachId) {
      alert('РќРµ РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅ С‚СЂРµРЅРµСЂ');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const response = await workoutService.createPlan({
        name: planName,
        coach_id: coachId
      });
      setPlanId(response.data.id);
      
      for (let i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {
        await workoutService.addDay(response.data.id, i);
      }
      
      const daysResponse = await workoutService.getPlanDays(response.data.id);
      setDays(daysResponse.data.map((day: WorkoutDay) => ({
        number: day.day_number,
        exercises: day.exercises
          .filter((ex: any) => ex && ex.id)
          .map((ex: any) => ({
            ...ex,
            day_id: day.id
          }))
      })));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅРёСЏ РїР»Р°РЅР°:', error);
      alert('РћС€РёР±РєР° РїСЂРё СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅРёРё РїР»Р°РЅР°');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Р”РѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ РІ РґРµРЅСЊ
  const handleAddExercise = async (exercise: Exercise) => {
    if (!planId) return;

    try {
      const daysResponse = await workoutService.getPlanDays(planId);
      const currentDayData = daysResponse.data.find((d: WorkoutDay) => d.day_number === currentDay);
      if (!currentDayData) return;

      await workoutService.addExerciseToDay(currentDayData.id, {
        exercise_id: exercise.id,
        sets_count: 3,
        default_reps: 10,
        default_weight: null,  // в†ђ РјРµРЅСЏРµРј 0 РЅР° null
        order_index: days[currentDay-1]?.exercises.length || 0
      });

      const updatedDays = await workoutService.getPlanDays(planId);
      setDays(updatedDays.data.map((day: WorkoutDay) => ({
        number: day.day_number,
        exercises: day.exercises
          .filter((ex: any) => ex && ex.id)
          .map((ex: any) => ({
            ...ex,
            day_id: day.id
          }))
      })));
      
      setShowExerciseSelector(false);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° РґРѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅРёСЏ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ:', error);
    }
  };

  // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРµ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ
  const handleUpdateExercise = async (exerciseId: number, data: any) => {
    try {
      await workoutService.updateDayExercise(exerciseId, data);
      
      if (planId) {
        const daysResponse = await workoutService.getPlanDays(planId);
        setDays(daysResponse.data.map((day: WorkoutDay) => ({
          number: day.day_number,
          exercises: day.exercises
            .filter((ex: any) => ex && ex.id)
            .map((ex: any) => ({
              ...ex,
              day_id: day.id
            }))
        })));
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёСЏ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ:', error);
    }
  };

  // РЈРґР°Р»РµРЅРёРµ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ
  const handleDeleteExercise = async (exerciseId: number) => {
    if (!confirm('РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёРµ РёР· РґРЅСЏ?')) return;
    
    try {
      await workoutService.deleteDayExercise(exerciseId);
      
      if (planId) {
        const daysResponse = await workoutService.getPlanDays(planId);
        setDays(daysResponse.data.map((day: WorkoutDay) => ({
          number: day.day_number,
          exercises: day.exercises
            .filter((ex: any) => ex && ex.id)
            .map((ex: any) => ({
              ...ex,
              day_id: day.id
            }))
        })));
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° СѓРґР°Р»РµРЅРёСЏ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ:', error);
    }
  };

  // РџРµСЂРµРјРµС‰РµРЅРёРµ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ РІРІРµСЂС…
  const handleMoveUp = async (dayNumber: number, index: number) => {
    if (index === 0) return;
    
    const day = days.find(d => d.number === dayNumber);
    if (!day) return;
    
    const exercises = [...day.exercises];
    [exercises[index-1], exercises[index]] = [exercises[index], exercises[index-1]];
    
    if (planId && exercises[0]?.day_id) {
      try {
        const orderData = exercises.map((ex, i) => ({ id: ex.id, order_index: i }));
        await workoutService.reorderExercises(exercises[0].day_id, orderData);
        
        const updatedDays = [...days];
        const dayIndex = updatedDays.findIndex(d => d.number === dayNumber);
        if (dayIndex !== -1) {
          updatedDays[dayIndex].exercises = exercises;
          setDays(updatedDays);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° РїРµСЂРµРјРµС‰РµРЅРёСЏ:', error);
      }
    }
  };

  // РџРµСЂРµРјРµС‰РµРЅРёРµ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ РІРЅРёР·
  const handleMoveDown = async (dayNumber: number, index: number) => {
    const day = days.find(d => d.number === dayNumber);
    if (!day || index === day.exercises.length - 1) return;
    
    const exercises = [...day.exercises];
    [exercises[index], exercises[index+1]] = [exercises[index+1], exercises[index]];
    
    if (planId && exercises[0]?.day_id) {
      try {
        const orderData = exercises.map((ex, i) => ({ id: ex.id, order_index: i }));
        await workoutService.reorderExercises(exercises[0].day_id, orderData);
        
        const updatedDays = [...days];
        const dayIndex = updatedDays.findIndex(d => d.number === dayNumber);
        if (dayIndex !== -1) {
          updatedDays[dayIndex].exercises = exercises;
          setDays(updatedDays);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° РїРµСЂРµРјРµС‰РµРЅРёСЏ:', error);
      }
    }
  };

  // Р”РѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ РЅРѕРІРѕРіРѕ РґРЅСЏ
  const handleAddDay = async () => {
    if (!planId || days.length >= 10) return;
    
    try {
      await workoutService.addDay(planId, days.length + 1);
      const daysResponse = await workoutService.getPlanDays(planId);
      setDays(daysResponse.data.map((day: WorkoutDay) => ({
        number: day.day_number,
        exercises: day.exercises
          .filter((ex: any) => ex && ex.id)
          .map((ex: any) => ({
            ...ex,
            day_id: day.id
          }))
      })));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° РґРѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅРёСЏ РґРЅСЏ:', error);
    }
  };

  // РќР°Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµ РїР»Р°РЅР° СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР°Рј
  const handleAssignPlans = async () => {
    if (!planId || selectedAthletes.length === 0) {
      alert('Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅРѕРІ');
      return;
    }

    try {
      for (const athleteId of selectedAthletes) {
        await workoutService.assignToAthlete(planId, athleteId);
      }
      alert('РџР»Р°РЅ СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕ РЅР°Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР°Рј!');
      setShowAthleteSelector(false);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° РЅР°Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёСЏ РїР»Р°РЅР°:', error);
      alert('РћС€РёР±РєР° РїСЂРё РЅР°Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРё РїР»Р°РЅР°');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="create-plan-page">
      <div className="page-header">
        <button className="back-btn" onClick={() => navigate('/coach/dashboard')}>
          <ArrowLeft size={20} />
        </button>
        <h1>РЎРѕР·РґР°РЅРёРµ РїР»Р°РЅР°</h1>
      </div>

      {!planId ? (
        <div className="plan-setup">
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="РќР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ РїР»Р°РЅР° (РЅР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ: РњРµСЃСЏС† 1 - РќР°С‡Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ СѓСЂРѕРІРµРЅСЊ)"
            value={planName}
            onChange={(e) => setPlanName(e.target.value)}
            className="plan-name-input"
          />
          <button 
            className="create-btn"
            onClick={handleCreatePlan}
            disabled={loading}
          >
            <Save size={20} />
            {loading ? 'РЎРѕР·РґР°РЅРёРµ...' : 'РЎРѕР·РґР°С‚СЊ РїР»Р°РЅ'}
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="days-tabs">
            {days.map((day) => (
              <button
                key={day.number}
                className={`day-tab ${currentDay === day.number ? 'active' : ''}`}
                onClick={() => setCurrentDay(day.number)}
              >
                Р”РµРЅСЊ {day.number}
              </button>
            ))}
            {days.length < 10 && (
              <button className="add-day-btn" onClick={handleAddDay}>
                <Plus size={18} />
                Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ РґРµРЅСЊ
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="day-content">
            <div className="exercises-list">
              {days[currentDay-1]?.exercises.map((exercise, index) => (
                <PlanExerciseItem
                  key={exercise.id}
                  exercise={exercise}
                  onUpdate={handleUpdateExercise}
                  onDelete={handleDeleteExercise}
                  onMoveUp={() => handleMoveUp(currentDay, index)}
                  onMoveDown={() => handleMoveDown(currentDay, index)}
                  showMoveButtons={days[currentDay-1]?.exercises.length > 1}
                />
              ))}

              <button
                className="add-exercise-btn"
                onClick={() => setShowExerciseSelector(true)}
              >
                <Plus size={20} />
                Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёРµ
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="plan-actions">
            <button
              className="assign-btn"
              onClick={() => setShowAthleteSelector(true)}
            >
              <Users size={20} />
              РќР°Р·РЅР°С‡РёС‚СЊ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР°Рј
            </button>
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {showExerciseSelector && (
        <ExerciseSelector
          onSelect={handleAddExercise}
          onClose={() => setShowExerciseSelector(false)}
          selectedExerciseIds={days[currentDay-1]?.exercises.map(ex => ex.exercise_id) || []}
        />
      )}

      {showAthleteSelector && (
        <div className="selector-overlay" onClick={() => setShowAthleteSelector(false)}>
          <div className="selector-content" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
            <div className="selector-header">
              <h2>Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅРѕРІ</h2>
              <button className="close-btn" onClick={() => setShowAthleteSelector(false)}>
                <ArrowLeft size={20} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="athlete-list">
              {athletes.map(athlete => (
                <label key={athlete.id} className="athlete-checkbox">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={selectedAthletes.includes(athlete.id)}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      if (e.target.checked) {
                        setSelectedAthletes([...selectedAthletes, athlete.id]);
                      } else {
                        setSelectedAthletes(selectedAthletes.filter(id => id !== athlete.id));
                      }
                    }}
                  />
                  <span>{athlete.first_name} {athlete.last_name}</span>
                </label>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="selector-actions">
              <button className="cancel-btn" onClick={() => setShowAthleteSelector(false)}>
                РћС‚РјРµРЅР°
              </button>
              <button className="save-btn" onClick={handleAssignPlans}>
                РќР°Р·РЅР°С‡РёС‚СЊ
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

=== FILE: F:\Projects\fitness-app\frontend\src\pages\ExerciseLibrary.tsx ===
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Search, Plus, Filter, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import exerciseService from '../services/exerciseService';
import type { Exercise } from '../services/exerciseService';
import { ExerciseCard } from '../components/ExerciseCard';
import { ExerciseModal } from '../components/ExerciseModal';
import './ExerciseLibrary.css';

export const ExerciseLibrary: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [exercises, setExercises] = useState<Exercise[]>([]);
  const [filteredExercises, setFilteredExercises] = useState<Exercise[]>([]);
  const [muscleGroups, setMuscleGroups] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [selectedGroup, setSelectedGroup] = useState<string>('');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [modalOpen, setModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingExercise, setEditingExercise] = useState<Exercise | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    filterExercises();
  }, [exercises, selectedGroup, searchQuery]);

  const loadData = async () => {
    try {
      const [exercisesRes, groupsRes] = await Promise.all([
        exerciseService.getAll(),
        exerciseService.getMuscleGroups()
      ]);
      setExercises(exercisesRes.data.exercises);
      setMuscleGroups(groupsRes.data);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё:', error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const filterExercises = () => {
    let filtered = [...exercises];
    
    if (selectedGroup) {
      filtered = filtered.filter(ex => ex.muscle_group === selectedGroup);
    }
    
    if (searchQuery) {
      filtered = filtered.filter(ex => 
        ex.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())
      );
    }
    
    setFilteredExercises(filtered);
  };

  const handleCreateExercise = async (exerciseData: any) => {
    try {
      await exerciseService.create({
        ...exerciseData,
        created_by_coach_id: 1 // Р’СЂРµРјРµРЅРЅРѕ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј ID С‚СЂРµРЅРµСЂР° = 1
      });
      await loadData();
      setModalOpen(false);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРѕР·РґР°РЅРёСЏ:', error);
    }
  };

  const handleUpdateExercise = async (exerciseData: any) => {
    if (!editingExercise) return;
    try {
      await exerciseService.update(editingExercise.id, exerciseData);
      await loadData();
      setModalOpen(false);
      setEditingExercise(null);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёСЏ:', error);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteExercise = async (id: number) => {
    if (window.confirm('РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ СЌС‚Рѕ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёРµ?')) {
      try {
        await exerciseService.delete(id);
        await loadData();
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° СѓРґР°Р»РµРЅРёСЏ:', error);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleEdit = (exercise: Exercise) => {
    setEditingExercise(exercise);
    setModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleModalClose = () => {
    setModalOpen(false);
    setEditingExercise(null);
  };

  return (
    <div className="exercise-library">
      <div className="library-header">
        <button className="back-btn" onClick={() => navigate('/coach/dashboard')}>
          <ArrowLeft size={20} />
        </button>
        <h1>Р‘РёР±Р»РёРѕС‚РµРєР° СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёР№</h1>
        <button className="add-btn" onClick={() => setModalOpen(true)}>
          <Plus size={20} />
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="filters-section">
        <div className="search-box">
          <Search size={20} />
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="РџРѕРёСЃРє СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёР№..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="group-filter">
          <Filter size={20} />
          <select 
            value={selectedGroup} 
            onChange={(e) => setSelectedGroup(e.target.value)}
          >
            <option value="">Р’СЃРµ РіСЂСѓРїРїС‹</option>
            {muscleGroups.map(group => (
              <option key={group} value={group}>{group}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <p className="loading">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°...</p>
      ) : (
        <div className="exercises-list">
          {filteredExercises.length > 0 ? (
            filteredExercises.map(exercise => (
              <ExerciseCard
                key={exercise.id}
                exercise={exercise}
                onEdit={handleEdit}
                onDelete={handleDeleteExercise}
              />
            ))
          ) : (
            <p className="empty-state">
              {searchQuery || selectedGroup 
                ? 'РќРёС‡РµРіРѕ РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅРѕ' 
                : 'РЈРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёР№ РїРѕРєР° РЅРµС‚. РЎРѕР·РґР°Р№С‚Рµ РїРµСЂРІРѕРµ!'}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      <ExerciseModal
        isOpen={modalOpen}
        onClose={handleModalClose}
        onSave={editingExercise ? handleUpdateExercise : handleCreateExercise}
        exercise={editingExercise}
        muscleGroups={muscleGroups}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Users, Dumbbell, ArrowLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import api from '../services/api';
import './UserSelectionPage.css';

interface User {
  id: number;
  first_name: string;
  last_name: string;
  role: 'coach' | 'athlete' | 'admin';
  telegram_id?: string;
  phone?: string;
  coach_id?: number;
}

export const UserSelectionPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [role, setRole] = useState<'coach' | 'athlete' | null>(null);
  const [users, setUsers] = useState<User[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (role) {
      loadUsers();
    }
  }, [role]);

  const loadUsers = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const response = await api.get('/users');
      const allUsers = response.data.data || [];
      // Admin users with coach_id also appear in the athlete list
      const filteredUsers = allUsers.filter((u: User) =>
        u.role === role || (role === 'athlete' && u.role === 'admin' && u.coach_id != null)
      );
      setUsers(filteredUsers);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»РµР№:', error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleUserSelect = (user: User) => {
    if (user.role === 'coach') {
      localStorage.setItem('selectedCoachId', user.id.toString());
      navigate('/coach/dashboard');
    } else {
      localStorage.setItem('selectedAthleteId', user.id.toString());
      navigate('/athlete/dashboard');
    }
  };

  if (!role) {
    return (
      <div className="user-selection-page">
        <div className="role-selection">
          <h1>РљС‚Рѕ РІС‹?</h1>
          <div className="role-buttons">
            <button className="role-btn coach" onClick={() => setRole('coach')}>
              <Users size={48} />
              <span>РўСЂРµРЅРµСЂ</span>
            </button>
            <button className="role-btn athlete" onClick={() => setRole('athlete')}>
              <Dumbbell size={48} />
              <span>РЎРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅ</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="user-selection-page">
      <div className="user-list-container">
        <div className="user-list-header">
          <button className="back-btn" onClick={() => setRole(null)}>
            <ArrowLeft size={20} />
          </button>
          <h1>{role === 'coach' ? 'Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ С‚СЂРµРЅРµСЂР°' : 'Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР°'}</h1>
        </div>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="loading">Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°...</div>
        ) : users.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="user-list">
            {users.map((user) => (
              <div
                key={user.id}
                className="user-card"
                onClick={() => handleUserSelect(user)}
              >
                <div className="user-avatar">
                  ID:{user.id}
                </div>
                <div className="user-info">
                  <h3>{user.first_name} {user.last_name}</h3>
                </div>
                <div className="user-card-arrow">
                  <ChevronRight size={18} />
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="empty-state">
            <p>РќРµС‚ {role === 'coach' ? 'С‚СЂРµРЅРµСЂРѕРІ' : 'СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅРѕРІ'}</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import axios from 'axios';
import { API_BASE_URL } from '../config';

const api = axios.create({
  baseURL: API_BASE_URL,
  headers: {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
  },
});

api.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => response,
  (error) => {
    console.error('API Error:', error.response?.data || error.message);
    return Promise.reject(error);
  }
);

export default api;

    // РџРѕРєР° РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј Р·Р°РіР»СѓС€РєСѓ - РїРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµРј РїР»Р°РЅС‹ С‚СЂРµРЅРµСЂР°

      console.log('РќР°С‡Р°Р»Рѕ С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєРё:', { athleteId, planId, dayId });
      const response = await api.post('/logs/sessions/start', {
        athlete_id: athleteId,
        plan_id: planId,
        day_id: dayId
      });
      console.log('РћС‚РІРµС‚ СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂР°:', response.data);
      return response.data;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° РїСЂРё СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚Рµ С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєРё:', error);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  // РџРѕР»СѓС‡РµРЅРёРµ Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅРѕР№ С‚СЂРµРЅРёСЂРѕРІРєРё
  async getActiveWorkout(athleteId: number) {

    return response.data;

  async getAll(muscleGroup?: string) {

      ? `/exercises?muscleGroup=${encodeURIComponent(muscleGroup)}`
      : '/exercises';
    const response = await api.get(url);

    const response = await api.get(`/exercises/coach/${coachId}`);
    return response.data;
  }

  // РџРѕР»СѓС‡РµРЅРёРµ РІСЃРµС… РіСЂСѓРїРї РјС‹С€С†
  async getMuscleGroups() {
    const response = await api.get('/exercises/muscle-groups');
    return response.data;
  }

  // РЎРѕР·РґР°РЅРёРµ РЅРѕРІРѕРіРѕ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ
  async create(data: CreateExerciseData) {
    const response = await api.post('/exercises', data);
    return response.data;
  }

  // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРµ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ
  async update(id: number, data: Partial<CreateExerciseData>) {
    const response = await api.put(`/exercises/${id}`, data);
    return response.data;
  }

  // РЈРґР°Р»РµРЅРёРµ СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёСЏ
  async delete(id: number) {
    const response = await api.delete(`/exercises/${id}`);
    return response.data;
  }

  // РџРѕРёСЃРє СѓРїСЂР°Р¶РЅРµРЅРёР№
  async search(query: string) {
    const response = await api.get(`/exercises/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`);
    return response.data;
  }
}

// в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
const NotRegisteredScreen: React.FC<{ telegramId?: string | null }> = ({ telegramId }) => {
  const [code, setCode]     = useState('');
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'loading' | 'error'>('idle');
  const [errMsg, setErrMsg] = useState('');

  // РР·РІР»РµРєР°РµС‚ С‡РёСЃР»Рѕ РёР· СЃС‚СЂРѕРє РІРёРґР°: "6", "athlete_6",
  // "https://t.me/bot?startapp=athlete_6", "athlete_6"
  const parseAthleteId = (raw: string): number | null => {
    const clean = raw.trim();
    const m = clean.match(/athlete[_-]?(\d+)/i) ?? clean.match(/(\d+)/);
    return m ? parseInt(m[1], 10) : null;
  };

  const handleLink = async () => {
    if (!telegramId) {
      setErrMsg('Telegram ID РЅРµ РѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»С‘РЅ. РћС‚РєСЂРѕР№С‚Рµ РїСЂРёР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёРµ С‡РµСЂРµР· Р±РѕС‚Р°.');
      setStatus('error');
      return;
    }
    const athleteId = parseAthleteId(code);
    if (!athleteId) {
      setErrMsg('РќРµРІРµСЂРЅС‹Р№ РєРѕРґ. Р’СЃС‚Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ СЃСЃС‹Р»РєСѓ-РїСЂРёРіР»Р°С€РµРЅРёРµ РѕС‚ С‚СЂРµРЅРµСЂР°.');
      setStatus('error');
      return;
    }

    setStatus('loading');
    try {
      const API = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL ?? 'https://fitness-app-production-33d3.up.railway.app/api';
      const res = await fetch(`${API}/auth/telegram/link`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ telegramId, userId: athleteId }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        throw new Error(data.message ?? `РћС€РёР±РєР° ${res.status}`);
      }
      // РћС‚РєСЂС‹РІР°РµРј Р±РѕС‚Р° РІ Telegram вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ РґРѕР±Р°РІРёС‚ РµРіРѕ РІ СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє С‡Р°С‚РѕРІ
      // Рё СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅ СЃРјРѕР¶РµС‚ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹РІР°С‚СЊ РїСЂРёР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёРµ РІ Р»СЋР±РѕРµ РІСЂРµРјСЏ
      const tg = (window as any).Telegram?.WebApp;
      if (tg?.openTelegramLink) {
        tg.openTelegramLink('https://t.me/kablaev_team_bot?start=joined');
      } else {
        window.location.reload();
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      setErrMsg(e.message ?? 'РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂР°');
      setStatus('error');
    }
  };

  const s = {
    page: {
      display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' as const,
      alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
      minHeight: '100vh', gap: 16, padding: 24,
      background: 'var(--fit-bg, #0a0a0c)',
      color: 'var(--fit-text, #f4f4f5)',
      fontFamily: 'system-ui, sans-serif',
      textAlign: 'center' as const,
    },
    card: {
      width: '100%', maxWidth: 340,
      background: '#18181b', border: '1px solid #27272a',
      borderRadius: 16, padding: 20,
      display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' as const, gap: 12,
    },
    input: {
      width: '100%', padding: '12px 14px',
      background: '#0a0a0c', border: '1px solid #27272a',
      borderRadius: 10, color: '#f4f4f5',
      fontSize: 14, outline: 'none',
      boxSizing: 'border-box' as const,
    },
    btn: {
      width: '100%', padding: '13px 0',
      background: status === 'loading' ? '#4d6e1a' : '#a3e635',
      color: '#0a0a0c', border: 'none',
      borderRadius: 10, fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700,
      cursor: status === 'loading' ? 'default' : 'pointer',
    },
  };

  return (
    <div style={s.page}>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 48 }}>рџЏ‹пёЏ</div>
      <h2 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 20, fontWeight: 700 }}>РђРєРєР°СѓРЅС‚ РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ</h2>
      <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 14, color: 'var(--fit-muted, #a1a1aa)', lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
        Р’СЃС‚Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ СЃСЃС‹Р»РєСѓ РёР»Рё РєРѕРґ, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Р№ РїСЂРёСЃР»Р°Р» С‚СЂРµРЅРµСЂ
      </p>

      <div style={s.card}>
        <label style={{ fontSize: 13, color: '#a1a1aa', textAlign: 'left' }}>
          Р’СЃС‚Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ Р·РґРµСЃСЊ
        </label>
        <input
          style={s.input}
          placeholder="https://t.me/kablaev_team_bot?startapp=athlete_6"
          value={code}
          onChange={e => { setCode(e.target.value); setStatus('idle'); }}
        />
        {status === 'error' && (
          <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 13, color: '#f87171', textAlign: 'left' }}>{errMsg}</p>
        )}
        <button style={s.btn} onClick={handleLink} disabled={status === 'loading'}>
          {status === 'loading' ? 'РџРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёРµвЂ¦' : 'Р’РѕР№С‚Рё'}
        </button>
      </div>

      {telegramId && (
        <p style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#52525b', margin: 0 }}>
          Р’Р°С€ Telegram ID: <strong style={{ color: '#a3e635' }}>{telegramId}</strong>
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

// в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
// Р—Р°С‰РёС‰С‘РЅРЅС‹Р№ РјР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚ вЂ” РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµС‚ СЂРѕР»СЊ РёР· AuthContext
// в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
const RequireRole: React.FC<{
  role: 'coach' | 'athlete' | 'admin';
  children: React.ReactNode;
}> = ({ role, children }) => {
  const { authUser, authStatus } = useAuth();

  if (authStatus === 'loading') return <LoadingScreen />;
  if (!authUser) return <Navigate to="/" replace />;
  // РђРґРјРёРЅРёСЃС‚СЂР°С‚РѕСЂ РёРјРµРµС‚ РґРѕСЃС‚СѓРї Рє Р»СЋР±РѕРјСѓ РјР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚Сѓ
  if (authUser.role !== role && authUser.role !== 'admin') return <Navigate to="/" replace />;
  return <>{children}</>;
};

// в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
// РљРѕСЂРЅРµРІРѕР№ РјР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚ вЂ” СЂРѕСѓС‚РёРЅРі РїРѕ СЂРѕР»Рё
// в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
const RootRedirect: React.FC = () => {
  const { authUser, authStatus } = useAuth();
  const { telegramId } = useTelegram();

  if (authStatus === 'loading') return <LoadingScreen message="РђРІС‚РѕСЂРёР·Р°С†РёСЏвЂ¦" />;

  if (authStatus === 'not_found') return <NotRegisteredScreen telegramId={telegramId} />;
  if (authStatus === 'error')    return <NotRegisteredScreen telegramId={telegramId} />;

  if (!authUser) return <NotRegisteredScreen />;

  switch (authUser.role) {
    case 'coach':   return <Navigate to="/coach/dashboard"   replace />;
    case 'athlete': return <Navigate to="/athlete/dashboard" replace />;
    case 'admin':   return <Navigate to="/admin/dashboard"   replace />;
    default:        return <NotRegisteredScreen />;
  }
};

// в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
// Р РѕСѓС‚РµСЂ
// в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
function AppRoutes() {
  return (
    <Router>
      <Routes>
        {/* РљРѕСЂРµРЅСЊ вЂ” РѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»СЏРµС‚ РєСѓРґР° РёРґС‚Рё РїРѕ СЂРѕР»Рё */}
        <Route path="/" element={<RootRedirect />} />

        {/* РњР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚С‹ С‚СЂРµРЅРµСЂР° */}
        <Route path="/coach/dashboard" element={
          <RequireRole role="coach"><CoachDashboard /></RequireRole>
        } />
        <Route path="/coach/exercises" element={
          <RequireRole role="coach"><ExerciseLibrary /></RequireRole>
        } />
        <Route path="/coach/create-plan" element={
          <RequireRole role="coach"><CreatePlanPage /></RequireRole>
        } />
        <Route path="/coach/athlete/:athleteId/calendar" element={
          <RequireRole role="coach"><CoachAthleteCalendarPage /></RequireRole>
        } />
        <Route path="/coach/athlete/:athleteId/progress" element={
          <RequireRole role="coach"><CoachAthleteProgressPage /></RequireRole>
        } />
        <Route path="/coach/athlete/:athleteId/plans" element={
          <RequireRole role="coach"><CoachAthletePlansPage /></RequireRole>
        } />
        <Route path="/coach/edit-plan/:planId" element={
          <RequireRole role="coach"><CoachEditPlanPage /></RequireRole>
        } />

        {/* РњР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚С‹ СЃРїРѕСЂС‚СЃРјРµРЅР° */}
        <Route path="/athlete/dashboard" element={
          <RequireRole role="athlete"><AthleteDashboard /></RequireRole>
        } />
        <Route path="/athlete/plan/:planId" element={
          <RequireRole role="athlete"><AthletePlanPage /></RequireRole>
        } />
        <Route path="/athlete/workout/:planId/day/:dayId" element={
          <RequireRole role="athlete"><AthleteWorkoutPage /></RequireRole>
        } />
        <Route path="/athlete/complete" element={
          <RequireRole role="athlete"><AthleteCompletePage /></RequireRole>
        } />
        <Route path="/athlete/calendar" element={
          <RequireRole role="athlete"><AthleteCalendarPage /></RequireRole>
        } />
        <Route path="/athlete/progress" element={
          <RequireRole role="athlete"><AthleteProgressPage /></RequireRole>
        } />

        {/* РџР°РЅРµР»СЊ Р°РґРјРёРЅРёСЃС‚СЂР°С‚РѕСЂР° */}
        <Route path="/admin/dashboard" element={
          <RequireRole role="admin"><AdminDashboard /></RequireRole>
        } />

        {/* РЎС‚Р°СЂС‚РѕРІС‹Р№ СЌРєСЂР°РЅ (legacy) */}
        <Route path="/home" element={<HomePage />} />

        {/* РЎР»СѓР¶РµР±РЅС‹Рµ */}
        <Route path="/select-user" element={<UserSelectionPage />} />
        <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/" replace />} />
      </Routes>
    </Router>
  );
}

function App() {
  const { isReady, telegramId } = useTelegram();

  if (!isReady) return <LoadingScreen />;

  return (
    <AppRoot>
      <AuthProvider telegramId={telegramId}>
        <AppRoutes />
      </AuthProvider>
    </AppRoot>
  );
}

export default App;
